# Änderungsprotokoll 0.10.16: Die anderen Dienste, Schattenoffiziere und Nachläufe

Erzeugt aus dem Vergleich der Kompendien mit dem Stand 0.10.15 (main), absatzweise. Gezeigt werden `odin-abenteuer(-en)`, `odin-gegner(-en)` und die
Beschreibungen in `odin-kraefte(-en)` und `odin-zauber(-en)`; die Kopien in `odin-abenteuerpakete(-en)` haben denselben Text (geprüft).
Quellen: Freigabelisten in diesem Ordner (Einträge mit „ja“) und von Hand gerichtete Stellen, bei denen das Werkzeug nicht ersetzen konnte
(Formatierung, Verweise) oder ein Eintrag Wörter verloren bzw. einen Satz verdoppelt hätte; Wortlaut dann nach dem Band.

Lesehilfen nur in diesem Protokoll, nicht in den Packs: „¶“ steht für einen Absatz- oder Zeilenwechsel innerhalb eines Eintrags, „Zahlen: {…}“ fasst die Zahlenfelder eines Gegners zusammen (i Initiative, v Verteidigung, r Rüstung, lp LP, g Grauen, p Pools als Name weiß+bunt/Schaden).

## Journale Deutsch (12 Absätze)

### 11 Die Erleuchteten › Köpfe

- alt: Angriff Pfefferspray 2 + 2, Schaden 1, Blendet
- neu: Angriff Pfefferspray 2 + 2, kein Schaden, das Ziel ist eine Runde lang geblendet (−2 Würfel)

### 06 Abteilung 13 › Stützpunkte und Mittel

- alt: Sperrzone „Kola-7“. Halbinsel Kola, etwa 80 Kilometer südöstlich von Murmansk, in einem stillgelegten Bergwerk aus der Zarenzeit. Offiziell ist das Gebiet wegen Einsturzgefahr gesperrt. Dort vermutet die Abteilung die Ader aus Legierungserz, auf die 2019 die ersten Messungen deuteten, dazu ein Lager mit Wohncontainern, eine Werkstatt für die Golems und ein Schacht, der in die Tiefe führt, in der die Ader liegt. Vor Ort sind etwa dreißig Arbeiter und Techniker, zehn Jäger, zwei Psionen ohne Helm und ein Golem-7, der den Eingang bewacht (Werte im Bedrohungsatlas). Hinein kommt man über eine unbefestigte Straße, die bei Murmansk beginnt und an zwei Kontrollpunkten vorbeiführt, oder über den alten Schacht, der vom See aus zugänglich ist, wenn man tauchen kann.
- neu: Sperrzone „Kola-7“. Halbinsel Kola, etwa 80 Kilometer südöstlich von Murmansk, in einem stillgelegten Bergwerk aus der Zarenzeit. Offiziell ist das Gebiet wegen Einsturzgefahr gesperrt. Dort baut die Abteilung seit 2019 die Ader aus Legierungserz ab, dazu ein Lager mit Wohncontainern, eine Werkstatt für die Golems und ein Schacht, der in die Tiefe führt, in der die Ader liegt. Vor Ort sind etwa dreißig Arbeiter und Techniker, zehn Jäger, zwei Psionen ohne Helm und ein Golem-7, der den Eingang bewacht (Werte im Bedrohungsatlas). Hinein kommt man über eine unbefestigte Straße, die bei Murmansk beginnt und an zwei Kontrollpunkten vorbeiführt, oder über den alten Schacht, der vom See aus zugänglich ist, wenn man tauchen kann.

### 08 Die Partner › Was der Partner für eine Gefälligkeit verlangt

- alt: Ein Gefallen ist eine Währung, die zwischen der Zelle und einem Partnerdienst zirkuliert. Die Spielleitung notiert für jeden Partner, ob er der Zelle etwas schuldet oder umgekehrt. Einen notierten Gefallen einzulösen gelingt ohne Probe; verlangt die Zelle mehr, wird gewürfelt, zwei Stufen leichter. Ein Gefallen, den der Partner der Zelle schuldet, verfällt nach einem Kampagnenjahr. Ein Gefallen, den die Zelle schuldet, verfällt nie.
- neu: Ein Gefallen ist eine Währung, die zwischen der Zelle und einem Partnerdienst zirkuliert. Die Spielleitung notiert für jeden Partner, ob er der Zelle etwas schuldet oder umgekehrt. Einen notierten Gefallen einzulösen gelingt ohne Probe; verlangt die Zelle mehr, würfelt ein Agent CH + Verhandeln gegen die Schwierigkeit, die die Spielleitung für die Bitte festlegt, zwei Stufen leichter, höchstens bis Einfach (1). Ein Gefallen, den der Partner der Zelle schuldet, verfällt nach einem Kampagnenjahr. Ein Gefallen, den die Zelle schuldet, verfällt nie.

### 05 STILLWATER › Stützpunkte und Mittel

- alt: Artefakte: Die Kappe mit Kupferdraht aus den Siebzigern, die der Fernseher trägt. Sie ist ein Notbehelf, der die Resonanz dämpft, aber nicht aufhält. STILLWATER sucht seit Jahrzehnten nach der Wardenclyffe-Spule und dem Bullauge der Eldridge, ohne Erfolg.
- neu: Artefakte: Die Kappe mit Kupferdraht aus den Siebzigern, die der Fernseher trägt. Sie ist ein Notbehelf, der die Resonanz dämpft, aber nicht aufhält. STILLWATER will seit 1971 die Wardenclyffe-Spule aus Bern haben und sucht seit Jahrzehnten das Bullauge der Eldridge, ohne Erfolg.

### 05 STILLWATER › Geschichte und Ziele

- alt: 1961: Das Schleierprotokoll wird geschlossen. STILLWATER ist nicht Vertragspartei, aber die USA unterzeichnen stillschweigend. STILLWATER erhält den Auftrag, O.D.I.N. in Nordamerika zu beobachten, nicht zu bekämpfen.
- neu: 1961: Das Schleierprotokoll wird geschlossen. STILLWATER ist nicht Partei, aber die USA stimmen stillschweigend zu. STILLWATER erhält den Auftrag, O.D.I.N. in Nordamerika zu beobachten, nicht zu bekämpfen.

### 15 Der Abend › Stützpunkte und Mittel

- alt: Das Hotel wechselt mit jeder Auktion. In den letzten Jahren waren es das Hotel „Palais Achatmühle“ in Dresden, das „Hotel Cordula“ in Wien, das „Albergo Tre Campane“ in Bologna und das „Hotel Lanterna Nuova“ in Genua. Im Ballsaal stehen der Auktionstisch, Reihen vergoldeter Stühle und ein Podest mit Glasvitrine; an den Türen stehen Männer von Grey Meridian. Hinein kommt man nur mit einer Einladungskarte, deren Code am Eingang geprüft wird. Ein Infiltrator kann den Code fälschen (Fingerfertigkeit gegen Schwer (3)), ein Negotiator kann als Bieter auftreten, ein Spy kann sich ins Personal schleusen.
- neu: Das Hotel wechselt mit jeder Auktion. In den letzten Jahren waren es das Hotel „Palais Achatmühle“ in Dresden, das „Hotel Cordula“ in Wien, das „Albergo Tre Campane“ in Bologna und das „Hotel Lanterna Nuova“ in Genua. Im Ballsaal stehen der Auktionstisch, Reihen vergoldeter Stühle und ein Podest mit Glasvitrine; an den Türen stehen Männer von Grey Meridian. Hinein kommt man nur mit einer Einladungskarte, deren Code am Eingang geprüft wird. Ein Infiltrator kann den Code fälschen (Sicherheitssysteme gegen Schwer (3)), ein Negotiator kann als Bieter auftreten, ein Spy kann sich ins Personal schleusen.

### 04 Kleine Kulte › Die Erwartenden von Pilsen

- alt: Graue Akte: Auf einem alten Güterbahnhof in Pilsen verschwinden nachts Tiere. Ein Anwohner hat Blut auf den Gleisen gefunden. Die Zelle soll herausfinden, was dort lebt.
- neu: Graue Akte: Auf einem alten Güterbahnhof in Pilsen fällt nachts der Strom in einem genauen Kreis aus. Ein Anwohner hat Blut auf den Gleisen gefunden. Die Zelle soll herausfinden, was dort lebt.

### 04 Kleine Kulte › Verbindungen zwischen den Plänen

- alt: Erreicht Die offene Tür Stufe 4, sucht der Rückkehrer einen Kristall und findet die Spur nach Bayern. Das Erwachen des Steins gewinnt einen Totenkopf.
- neu: Erreicht Die offene Tür Stufe 4, sucht der Rückkehrer einen zweiten, größeren Kristall und findet die Spur nach Bayern. Das Erwachen des Steins gewinnt einen Totenkopf.

### 16 Grey Meridian › Grey Meridian in fremden Plänen

- alt: Wo eine Akte Grey Meridian einen Totenkopf gibt, etwa in Akte 3 „Nummer 341“ (Graue Akten), geht er an den Plan, dem die Firma gerade dient.
- neu: Wo eine Akte Grey Meridian einen Totenkopf gibt, geht er an den Plan, dem die Firma gerade dient.

### 02 Morrow Biologics › Stützpunkte und Mittel

- alt: Außenstelle Basel. Ein unscheinbares Labor in einem Gewerbegebiet am Rand von Basel, das offiziell einer Tochterfirma gehört. Hier lagert Morrow Proben, die nicht in Leiden sein sollen, und hier hat Dr. Brandsma 2018 seine heimliche Analyse durchführen lassen. Der Eingang ist eine Stahltür mit Zahlencode, die Kamera darüber ist eine Attrappe. Ein Saboteur mit Technik gegen Machbar (2) öffnet sie.
- neu: Außenstelle Basel. Ein unscheinbares Labor in einem Gewerbegebiet am Rand von Basel, das offiziell einer Tochterfirma gehört. Hier lagert Morrow Proben, die nicht in Leiden sein sollen, und hier hat Dr. Brandsma 2018 seine heimliche Analyse durchführen lassen. Der Eingang ist eine Stahltür mit Zahlencode, die Kamera darüber ist eine Attrappe. Ein Saboteur mit Sicherheitssysteme gegen Machbar (2) öffnet sie.

### 03 Die Sänger › Aufhänger und Ausblick

- alt: Graue Akte „Die Treppe“. In Rotterdam steht seit drei Monaten ein Auto im dritten Untergeschoss eines Parkhauses. Der Besitzer, Daniel Koslowski, ist verschwunden. Seine Frau Miriam ist ihm gefolgt und hat in der Nacht zuvor die Polizei angerufen und gesagt, sie habe ihn gefunden. Die Zelle soll das Parkhaus betreten, das vierte Untergeschoss finden und herausfinden, was dort gesungen wird.
- neu: Graue Akte „Die Treppe“. In Rotterdam steht seit drei Monaten ein Auto im dritten Untergeschoss eines Parkhauses. Der Besitzer, Daniel Koslowski, ist verschwunden. Seine Frau Miriam ist ihm gefolgt und hat in der Nacht zuvor die Polizei angerufen und gesagt, sie habe ihn gefunden. Die Zelle reist mit einer Genehmigung des Rates ein, wie in Akte 6 „Der Hauslehrer“ (Graue Akten). Sie soll das Parkhaus betreten, das vierte Untergeschoss finden und herausfinden, was dort gesungen wird.

### 03 Die Sänger › Geschichte und Ziele

- alt: 1989. Auf dem Brocken verschwinden 23 Menschen aus einer stillgelegten Abhörstation. Die Anwesenheitsliste vom 30. April 1989 umfasst 23 Personen, keine taucht je wieder auf. Eine Person trug den Nachnamen Wendt. O.D.I.N. stuft den Vorgang als ungeklärt ein, bis 2026 eine Zelle die Station betritt und die Treppe findet (Grundregelwerk, Prolog „Walpurgis“). Die Menschen auf der Treppe sind die 23 von 1989, aber sie sind nicht mehr dieselben. Sie sind durch einen Riss gegangen und als etwas anderes zurückgekehrt. Sie sprechen mit einer Stimme. Um sie ist ein Kult entstanden, ohne Anführer und ohne Buch.
- neu: 1989. Auf dem Brocken verschwinden 23 Menschen aus der Abhörstation, die heute stillgelegt ist. Die Anwesenheitsliste vom 30. April 1989 umfasst 23 Personen, keine taucht je wieder auf. Eine Person trug den Nachnamen Wendt. O.D.I.N. stuft den Vorgang als ungeklärt ein, bis 2026 eine Zelle die Station betritt und die Treppe findet (Grundregelwerk, Prolog „Walpurgis“). Die Menschen auf der Treppe sind die 23 von 1989, aber sie sind nicht mehr dieselben. Sie sind durch einen Riss gegangen und als etwas anderes zurückgekehrt. Sie sprechen mit einer Stimme. Um sie ist ein Kult entstanden, ohne Anführer und ohne Buch.

## Journale Englisch (402 Absätze)

### 04 Small Cults › The Sisters of the Singing Children

- alt: What they really have. A Psion without a helmet who does not know what he is. The Psion is a young man called Luca, 23, who lives with the group and believes he hears the saint. In reality he hears the Voice behind the Veil. He wears no helmet because he never received one. The group takes his gift for a miracle. Luca takes it for an illness.
- neu: What they really have. A Psion without a helmet who does not know what he is. The Psion is a young man named Luca, 23, who lives in the group and believes he hears the saint. In reality he hears the Voice behind the Veil. He wears no helmet because he never received one. The group takes his gift for a miracle. Luca takes it for an illness.

- alt: A 67-year-old woman who founded the group after she heard Luca singing. She believes he is a prophet. She carries a knife that she calls “the Mother’s Point”. She keeps quiet about the fact that she never lets Luca sleep, because she fears he might receive orders in his dreams that she does not know. Her voice is firm and quiet, like a teacher who never has to ask twice.
- neu: A 67-year-old woman who founded the group after she heard Luca singing. She believes he is a prophet. She carries a knife that she calls “the Mother’s Point”. She conceals that she never lets Luca sleep, because she fears he might receive commands in his dreams that she does not know. Her voice is firm and quiet, like a teacher who never has to ask twice.

- alt: A 23-year-old man who lives with the group and believes he hears the saint. In reality he hears the Voice behind the Veil. He wears no helmet because he never received one. He keeps quiet about the fact that he also hears the Voice when he is alone, and that it sometimes tells him things he does not understand. His voice is quiet and halting, as if he were apologising for every word.
- neu: A 23-year-old man who lives in the group and believes he hears the saint. In reality he hears the Voice behind the Veil. He wears no helmet because he never received one. - alt: Weakness The Voice. Whoever puts a helmet of Alloy S on him silences it ¶ Behaviour Reserved and fearful. He follows Sister Elena because she is the only one who tells him he is not mad
- neu: Weakness The Voice. Whoever puts a helmet made of Alloy S on him silences it ¶ Behaviour Reserved and anxious. He follows Sister Elena because she is the only one who tells him he is not mad

- alt: ☠☠○○○ The children’s parents bring them to the monastery because the Sisters promise healing. Luca hears them, and the Voice grows louder. The group begins to prepare a ritual to summon the saint.

- alt: White File: A village priest from Tuscany reports that two children from his parish were taken by their parents to a former monastery. The cell is to check whether this is a sect. The children sing in their sleep, and that is why O.D.I.N. is taking a look.

### 04 Small Cults › The Community of the Dreamer

- alt: Location. A block of flats in Duisburg-Marxloh. The group meets in the flat of an elderly woman on the fifth floor, always on Fridays at 9 pm. ¶ What they worship. A dead man who speaks in dreams. The members call him “the Dreamer”. He appears to them as a man in a grey coat who stands at a window and says sentences they do not understand. The group believes the Dreamer is a prophet who is showing them the way into a better world. They write his sentences down in school exercise books and compare them. ¶ What they really have. A haunt that is attached to an object: an old radio from the fifties that stands in the woman’s flat. The radio has been broken for years, but on some nights it crackles, and whoever falls asleep dreams of the man in the grey coat. The dead man is a former resident of the block who died in a fire in 1962. He does not want to prophesy. He wants someone to find his daughter, who moved away after the fire. The sentences the group hears are fragments of letters he never sent.
- neu: Location. A residential block in Duisburg-Marxloh. The group meets in the flat of an older woman on the fifth floor, always on Fridays at 9 p.m. ¶ What they worship. A dead man who speaks in dreams. The members call him “the Dreamer”. He appears to them as a man in a grey coat, standing at a window and saying sentences they do not understand. The group believes the Dreamer is a prophet who shows them the way to a better world. They write his sentences into exercise books and compare them. ¶ What they really have. A haunting attached to an object: an old radio from the 1950s that stands in the woman’s flat. The radio has been broken for years, but on some nights it crackles, and anyone who falls asleep dreams of the man in the grey coat. The dead man is a former resident of the block who died in a fire in 1962. He does not want to prophesy. He wants someone to find his daughter, who moved away after the fire. The sentences the group hears are fragments from letters he never sent.

- alt: A 58-year-old woman who first noticed the haunt and founded the group. She believes the Dreamer is a saint. She keeps quiet about the fact that she never sleeps in the flat, because she is afraid the Dreamer might command her to do something. Her voice is warm and tired, and she speaks of the dead man as of a guest one must not send away.
- neu: A 58-year-old woman who first noticed the haunting and founded the group. She believes the Dreamer is a saint. She keeps quiet about the fact that she never sleeps in the flat, because she is afraid the Dreamer might command her to do something. Her voice is warm and tired, and she speaks of the dead man as of a guest one must not send away.

- alt: The group consists of six to ten people from the neighbourhood, among them a baker, a teacher, an unemployed locksmith. Stats: Cultists (Core Rulebook), but they carry no weapons. They are no danger as long as no one touches the radio.
- neu: The group consists of six to ten people from the neighbourhood, including a baker, a teacher, an unemployed locksmith. Stats: Cultists (Core Rulebook), but they carry no weapons. They are no danger as long as nobody touches the radio.

- alt: ☠☠○○○ The ritual takes place, in a cellar beneath the block of flats. The haunt grows stronger. Other residents of the block dream of the man in the grey coat, even without the radio.
- neu: The ritual takes place in a cellar beneath the residential block. The haunt grows stronger. Other residents of the block dream of the man in the grey coat, even without the radio.

- alt: ☠☠☠☠○ Fatma Aydin prepares a second ritual, in the stairwell, with everyone who dreams. From the plan a Red File emerges. ¶ ☠☠☠☠☠ The haunt now belongs to the whole block. Every night all the residents dream the same dream. Whoever wakes up stands at the window and waits, and in the morning no one knows why. ¶ Key Figure. Fatma Aydin. If the cell takes her out, the plan loses two skulls. Without her the group finds no place for the ritual.
- neu: ☠☠☠☠○ Fatma Aydin prepares a second ritual, in the stairwell, with everyone who dreams. From the plan, a Red File is created. ¶ ☠☠☠☠☠ The haunt now belongs to the whole block. ### 04 Small Cults › The Awaiting Ones of Pilsen

- alt: Location. A former goods station in Pilsen, Czech Republic. The group meets in an old railway carriage that stands on a siding.
- neu: Location. A former goods station in Pilsen, Czechia. The group meets in an old wagon standing on a siding.

- alt: What they really have. Contact with a being that answers. The being is not an extraterrestrial but the Spectator (Threat Atlas), which lives in a disused sewer beneath the goods station. It answers radio signals that the group sends with an old radio set. The Spectator does not understand what the group wants. It answers because the signals call it, and it wants to come across to see. The group takes its answers for messages from the Messenger.
- neu: What they really have. Contact with a being that answers. The being is not an extraterrestrial but the Spectator (Threat Atlas), which lives in a disused sewer beneath the goods station. It answers radio signals that the group sends with an old radio set. The Spectator does not understand what the group wants. It answers because the signals call it, and it wants to come over to see. The group takes its answers for messages from the Messenger.

- alt: A former radio amateur, 44 years old, who built the radio set and made the first contact. He believes the Messenger chose him. He wears a home-made helmet of copper wire that shields nothing but is supposed to protect him from “false thoughts”. He keeps quiet about the fact that he sits alone in the carriage at night and waits for an answer that does not come. His voice is quick and jittery, as if he had to say everything before the contact breaks off.
- neu: A former radio amateur, 44 years old, who built the radio set and made first contact. He believes the Messenger has chosen him. He wears a homemade helmet of copper wire that shields nothing but is meant to protect him from “false thoughts”. He keeps quiet about the fact that he sits alone in the carriage at night and waits for an answer that does not come. His voice is quick and erratic, as if he must say everything before the contact breaks off.

- alt: The group consists of five to eight people, among them a former railwayman, a shop assistant, a student. Stats: Cultists (Core Rulebook), but they carry no weapons. They are no danger as long as no one touches the radio set.
- neu: The group consists of five to eight people, including a former railwayman, a shop assistant, a student. Stats: Cultists (Core Rulebook), but they carry no weapons. They are no danger as long as nobody touches the radio set.

- alt: ☠○○○○ The group begins to prepare the goods station for the landing. It clears the site and paints circles on the ground. ¶ ☠☠○○○ The Spectator answers more often. At night it crawls through the sewer to beneath the goods station. The group hears it and believes the Messenger is near. ¶ ☠☠☠○○ At night the Spectator stands beneath the carriage and watches. The group takes it for the Messenger and lays gifts out for it. ¶ ☠☠☠☠○ Dvořák plans a transmission at full power for the night of the landing, to bring the Messenger into the light. From the plan a Red File emerges. ¶ ☠☠☠☠☠ The Spectator breaks through the floor of the carriage into the world. It is not a messenger but a being of the Alien, and it wants to see. Whoever stands in its way learns what else it can do.
- neu: ☠○○○○ The group begins to prepare the goods station for the landing. They clear the site and paint circles on the ground. ¶ ☠☠○○○ The Spectator answers more often. - alt: White File: A radio amateur from Pilsen reports that he is receiving a signal on one frequency that sounds like a voice. The cell is to check whether this is a rift. The signal comes from a disused sewer, and that is exactly what makes the file important. ¶ Grey File: At an old goods station in Pilsen, animals disappear at night. ### 04 Small Cults › Overview

- alt: Small cults are the everyday business of investigative work: groups that misunderstand something that is real, or worship something real that they do not understand. Most of them remain harmless. The following five are not, because each of them holds something in its hands that will grow without O.D.I.N. Each cult has its own summary line with relationship, strength, knowledge and plan.
- neu: Small cults are the everyday reality of investigation work: groups that misunderstand something real, or worship something real that they do not understand. Most of them remain harmless. The following five are not, because each of them holds something in their hands that grows without O.D.I.N. Each cult has its own short line with relationship, strength, knowledge and plan.

- alt: The game master uses small cults as adversaries that are closer to the players’ world than the large organisations. They are not armies and not conspiracies, but neighbours, believers, the confused. Their plans follow the rules of the skulls from the chapter “The World Behind the Veil”. After each mission the game master rolls a D6 for the cults that are currently important. On a 5 or 6, a skull is added. If the cell worked against the cult, the plan loses one skull, or two on a major success. At the second skull the cell receives a Grey File, at the fourth a Red one. At the fifth, what is stated in the plan happens. After that the cult gets a new plan, and the skulls start again from the beginning. The following five cults are built so that they work on their own or can be linked into a campaign.
- neu: The game master uses small cults as opponents who are closer to the players’ world than the large organisations. They are not armies and not conspiracies, but neighbours, believers, the confused. Their plans follow the skull rules from the chapter “The World Behind the Veil”. After every mission, the game master rolls a D6 for the cults that are currently important. On a 5 or 6, a skull is added. If the cell has worked against the cult, the plan loses one skull, or two after a major success. At the second skull the cell receives a Grey File, at the fourth a Red one. At the fifth, what is written in the plan happens. After that the cult gets a new plan, and the skulls start again from the beginning. The following five cults are built so that they work individually or can be linked into a campaign.

### 04 Small Cults › Connections Between the Plans

- alt: If The Choir of the Saints reaches level 4, the Returner wants Luca for his ritual, and The Open Door gains a skull. ¶ If The Open Door reaches level 4, the Returner looks for a crystal and finds the trail to Bavaria. The Awakening of the Stone gains a skull.
- neu: If The choir of the saints reaches stage 4, the Returned wants Luca for his ritual, and The open door gains a skull. ¶ If The open door reaches stage 4, the Returned looks for a second, larger crystal and finds the trail to Bavaria. The Awakening of the Stone gains a skull.

### 04 Small Cults › The Circle of the Returners

- alt: Location. A former office building in Antwerp, near the port. The group meets on the third floor, in a room overlooking the water. ¶ What they worship. A man who is called “the Returner”. He claims he went through a rift and came back. The members believe he knows what lies behind the Veil and can lead them there.
- neu: Location. A former office building in Antwerp, near the port. The group meets on the third floor, in a room with a view of the water. ¶ What they worship. A man called “the Returned”. He claims he went through a rift and came back. The members believe he knows what lies behind the Veil and can lead them there.

- alt: A 38-year-old man, a former O.D.I.N. agent, who disappeared in 2019 and came back in 2023. He remembers O.D.I.N.’s procedures, but not the names of the agents. He knows that there is a Seeker, but not who it is. He keeps quiet about the fact that he wakes up at night and does not know where he is. His voice is flat and calm, as if he were talking about someone else.

His voice is flat and calm, as if he were speaking about someone else.

- alt: Weakness His memory. Whoever proves to him that he is not chosen breaks his will
- neu: Weakness His memory. Anyone who proves to him that he is not chosen breaks his will

- alt: The group consists of ten to fifteen people, among them a dock worker, a translator, a former soldier. Stats: Fanatics (Threat Atlas), but they carry no weapons. They are dangerous because they believe everything the Returner says.

- alt: ☠○○○○ The group begins to search Antwerp for a place where the world is thin. The Returner remembers a cellar in which he disappeared in 2019.
- neu: ☠○○○○ The group begins searching Antwerp for a place where the world is thin. The Returner remembers a cellar in which he disappeared in 2019.

- alt: ☠☠☠○○ The group buys a crystal through the Evening. In the cellar it turns cold, and the clocks run slow. ¶ ☠☠☠☠○ The Returner lays the circle and sets the night. From the plan a Red File emerges. ¶ ☠☠☠☠☠ The ritual takes place. The rift opens, and the Returner goes through. This time he does not come back alone.

- alt: White File: A former O.D.I.N. agent who disappeared in Antwerp in 2019 has turned up again. The cell is to check whether he still works for the organisation. He remembers O.D.I.N., but not his name, and that is exactly what makes him dangerous. ¶ Grey File: In Antwerp a group is gathering around a man who claims he went through a rift. The cell is to find out what he really is.

The cell is to find out what he really is.

### 04 Small Cults › The Guardians of the Forest Crystal

- alt: Location. A village in the Bavarian Forest, near the Czech border. The group meets in a disused glassworks on the edge of the village, which has stood empty since 1998. ¶ What they worship. A fist-sized crystal that the founder found in the forest while gathering mushrooms in October 2023. The stone lies on a bed of dried moss in a tin box that the group calls “the Shrine”. The members believe the crystal is an “Eye of the Forest” that shows them where to dig. They call it “the Green One”. ¶ What they really have. A crystal from a rift find that was washed to the surface during a storm in 2023. The stone is a shard of a larger crystal that lies deeper in the forest floor. It answers touch with a hum that only the group hears. The hum is not a voice. It is the echo of a rift that opened for a few hours in 2023 and closed again. The crystal amplifies the growth of plants near it. Within three metres of the Shrine grow mushrooms that should not exist in the Bavarian Forest.
- neu: Location. A village in the Bavarian Forest, near the Czech border. The group meets in a disused glassworks on the edge of the village, empty since 1998. ¶ What they worship. A fist-sized crystal that the founder found while gathering mushrooms in the forest in October 2023. The stone lies on a bed of dried moss in a tin box that the group calls “the Shrine”. The members believe the crystal is an “Eye of the Forest” that shows them where to dig. They call it “the Green One”. ¶ What they really have. A crystal from a rift find, washed to the surface during a storm in 2023. The stone is a splinter of a larger crystal that lies deeper in the forest floor. It answers touch with a hum that only the group hears. The hum is not a voice. It is the echo of a rift that opened for a few hours in 2023 and closed again. The crystal accelerates the growth of plants near it. Within three metres of the Shrine, mushrooms grow that should not exist in the Bavarian Forest.

- alt: A former forester, 61 years old, who found the crystal and carries it in a leather pouch at every meeting. Hartl is convinced that the stone has chosen him. He talks to the crystal as if to an old friend. He keeps quiet about the fact that he goes to the glassworks alone at night and lays the stone on his bare skin to feel the hum. His voice is rough and quiet, as if he were talking to a sick animal.

- alt: Horror Uncanny (1), when he talks to the crystal
- neu: Horror Uncanny (1) when he speaks to the crystal

- alt: Weakness The crystal. Whoever destroys it or takes it away breaks Hartl’s will
- neu: Weakness The crystal. Whoever destroys or takes it breaks Hartl’s will

- alt: The group consists of eight to twelve people from the village and the neighbouring villages. They are not fighters, but glassmakers, farmers, a pharmacist. Stats: Cultists (Core Rulebook), but without weapons and without the special feature “never flees”. They flee when things get loud, unless Hartl orders them to stay.

- alt: ☠☠○○○ The large crystal is brought to the glassworks. It begins to warm the ground, and the plants in the surrounding area grow faster. A rift opens above the glassworks for one night.

- alt: ☠☠☠☠○ Hartl calls the group together at the solstice to open the rift for ever. From the plan a Red File emerges.
- neu: ☠☠☠☠○ Hartl calls the group together at the solstice to open the rift for ever. From the plan, a Red File is created.

- alt: White File: A forester from the Bavarian Forest reports that someone is digging holes in his district and filling them in again. The cell is to check whether these are crystal seekers. At one spot the forest floor is warm, and that is why the file lies with O.D.I.N. ¶ Grey File: In the disused glassworks near a village in the Bavarian Forest, plants are growing through the concrete floor. A local resident has taken photos. The cell is to find out what is growing there and why.

### 05 STILLWATER › History and Goals

- alt: 1954: STILLWATER is founded on the model of O.D.I.N., at first as a US Army programme for “psychological warfare”. The name comes from a disused water reservoir in Maryland, in whose maintenance tunnels the first experiments took place. The founders had access to crates of the Ahnenerbe that reached Washington after 1945.
- neu: 1954: STILLWATER is founded on the model of O.D.I.N., first as a US Army programme for “psychological warfare”. The name comes from a decommissioned water reservoir in Maryland, in whose maintenance tunnels the first experiments took place. The founders had access to Ahnenerbe crates that reached Washington after 1945.

- alt: 1974 to 1989: The publicly known US research into clairvoyance and remote viewing runs as the front. STILLWATER works behind it. In this period the programme finds its first psions of its own: adults whose gift awakened late and poorly. None of them is a Seeker. ¶ 1989: A copy of parts of the list from the Nursery turns up in Langley. To this day STILLWATER does not know who supplied it. The list contains the real names of Hands, escorts and foster families. STILLWATER uses it to put pressure on O.D.I.N. in North America. ¶ 2001: STILLWATER is separated from the CIA and moved into a cover agency that officially evaluates weather data. The programme loses budget, but not access. ¶ 2011: The new Seeker awakens. STILLWATER notices the accumulation of manifestations, but cannot explain it. The programme begins to search systematically for children who hear humming. ¶ 2025: An eleven-year-old boy in Fairbanks, Alaska, is discovered by Elaine Mercer on a family visit. The boy hears humming. STILLWATER believes it has found a Seeker of its own. What the programme does not know: the boy does not hear the Choir. He hears something else. ¶ What only the game master knows: Caleb Mercer does not only hear children. He also hears the voices of the 41 of Nordlicht, quietly, beneath everything else. Whether he is a Seeker or an echo of Nordlicht, no one knows, not even Dr. Webb. If STILLWATER opens him, America has its Seeker, and the European Seeker is the first to learn what else he hears besides the children.

During this time the programme finds its first own Psions: adults whose gift has awakened late and poorly. None of them is a Seeker. ¶ 1989: A copy of parts of the list from the Nursery appears in Langley. To this day STILLWATER does not know who supplied it. The list contains real names of Hands, companions and foster families. STILLWATER uses it to put pressure on O.D.I.N. in North America. ¶ 2001: STILLWATER is detached from the CIA and moved to a cover agency that officially evaluates weather data. The programme loses budget, but not access. ¶ 2011: The new Seeker awakens. STILLWATER notices the growing number of manifestations but cannot explain them. The programme begins to search systematically for children who hear humming. ¶ 2025: An eleven-year-old boy in Fairbanks, Alaska, is discovered by Elaine Mercer during a family visit. The boy hears humming. STILLWATER believes it has found a Seeker of its own. What the programme does not know: the boy does not hear the Choir. He hears something else. ¶ What only the game master knows: Caleb Mercer does not only hear children. He also hears the voices of the 41 from Nordlicht, quietly, beneath everything else. Whether he is a Seeker or an echo of Nordlicht, nobody knows, not even Dr. Webb. If STILLWATER opens him, America has its Seeker, and what he hears besides the children, the European Seeker learns first.

- alt: STILLWATER wants a Seeker of its own. The programme believes that O.D.I.N. only has so many psions because it possesses the Seeker. Without a Seeker, STILLWATER remains dependent on late-awakened adults whose gifts are weak and unreliable. With a Seeker of its own, so the Director is convinced, America could gain the same access to the Choir as Europe. ¶ STILLWATER believes it is in the right, because it believes that O.D.I.N. has a monopoly on the gift and abuses this monopoly. The programme sees itself as the only institution that can protect America from dependence on Europe. STILLWATER does not know that with every opening about every third child goes. Nor that Caleb Mercer is not a Seeker.

Nor that Caleb Mercer is not a Seeker.

### 05 STILLWATER › The Plan: “A Seeker of Their Own”

- alt: STILLWATER wants a Seeker of its own. The programme believes that O.D.I.N. only has so many psions because it possesses the Seeker. Without a Seeker, STILLWATER remains dependent on late-awakened adults whose gifts are weak and unreliable. The cell notices it first at the edges: a report from Alaska, a rumour in the canteen, a dead drop with a message that was not meant for it. Then the rumour becomes a file, and the file becomes a child.

Without a Seeker, STILLWATER remains dependent on late-awakened adults whose gifts are weak and unreliable. The cell first notices it at the edges: a report from Alaska, a rumour in the canteen, a dead drop with a message not meant for them. Then the rumour becomes a file, and the file becomes a child.

- alt: ☠○○○○ Elaine Mercer discovers Caleb Mercer in Fairbanks and reports him to the programme. Dr. Webb examines him and confirms: the boy hears something. Trail: A report about a boy in Alaska who does not sleep at night and talks about humming. The mother sees a doctor.

- alt: ☠☠☠○○ STILLWATER moves Caleb Mercer to the maintenance tunnels in Maryland. Dr. Webb begins preparations for the opening. Trail: A newspaper article about a boy from Fairbanks who has been accepted into a programme for gifted children. A psion of the cell hears a humming at night that does not come from O.D.I.N.

Sign: A newspaper article about a boy from Fairbanks who was accepted into a programme for highly gifted children. A psion of the cell hears a humming at night that does not come from O.D.I.N.

- alt: If the cell takes a skull from the plan, STILLWATER answers with a countermove.
- neu: If the cell takes a skull from the plan, the Foundation answers with a countermove.

- alt: 3 to 4 STILLWATER threatens to burn an O.D.I.N. agent in North America. A contact of the cell suddenly falls silent, a relay stops reporting. The cell learns that STILLWATER has the 1989 list. ¶ 5 to 6 STILLWATER moves Caleb Mercer to an unknown location. The cell loses the trail. A dead drop contains a message that was not meant for it: “The package is on its way.”

- alt: At ☠☠☠○○ : “Keep the Silence” (Congregation of the Silent Word) gains a skull. The Congregation learns of a second Seeker from a confession and fears that the silence can no longer be kept. ¶ At ☠☠☠☠☠ : “Taking the Children” (the Open Ones) gains a skull. The European Seeker hears the American one, and Kantor hears him too (Game Master’s Book, “Connecting Plans”).

The Congregation learns of a second Seeker from a confession and fears that the silence can no longer be kept. ¶ At ☠☠☠☠☠ : “Fetch the children” (the Open Ones) gains a skull. The European Seeker hears the American one, and Kantor hears him too (Game Master’s Book, “Connecting Plans”).

### 05 STILLWATER › Hooks and Outlook

- alt: Red File “The Package”. An informant in Boston reports that STILLWATER is holding a child in Maryland. The cell is to find the child before STILLWATER opens it. The trail leads to the maintenance tunnels in which Dr. Webb is preparing the opening.
- neu: Red File “The Package”. An informant in Boston reports that STILLWATER is holding a child in Maryland. The cell is to find the child before STILLWATER opens it. The trail leads to the maintenance tunnels where Dr. Webb is preparing the opening.

- alt: Caleb Mercer has become America’s own Seeker. The European Seeker hears a second tone for the first time, very far away, in North America. It sounds like a Seeker. O.D.I.N. must decide what to do with a second Seeker: whether to observe him, whether to try to understand him, or whether to eliminate him. STILLWATER now has what it wanted, and does not know what it will set off with it.
- neu: Caleb Mercer has become America’s own Seeker. The European Seeker hears a second tone for the first time, very far away, in North America. It sounds like a Seeker. O.D.I.N. must decide what to do with a second Seeker: whether to observe him, whether to try to understand him, or whether to eliminate him. STILLWATER now has what it wanted and does not know what it will cause with it.

### 05 STILLWATER › Bases and Means

- alt: The National Atmospheric Data Center, Washington, D.C. An inconspicuous office building in the city centre, six storeys, grey façade. Officially an archive for historical weather data. It houses STILLWATER’s directorate, the programme’s registry, a small server room and a conference room with maps of North America on which O.D.I.N. movements are marked. Elaine Mercer, Dr. Marcus Webb and about twenty employees work there, officially evaluating weather data and unofficially writing reports on O.D.I.N. You get in with a service ID from the cover agency. The security check is thorough, but not insurmountable. An Infiltrator with a good legend gets in. A psion with a helmet is not recognised at the entrance, but the surveillance cameras record everything. ¶ The maintenance tunnels, Maryland. A disused water reservoir in Maryland, about an hour from Washington. The tunnels are dry and have been used by STILLWATER since 1954. They house STILLWATER’s Psionics Department: a laboratory, a room with copper-wire cages in which psions without helmets are examined, and an archive of the Remote Viewer’s drawings. Dr. Marcus Webb, two technicians and three psions who are no longer fit for duty work there. You get in through a locked maintenance shaft at the edge of the reservoir. The entrance is camouflaged and watched by cameras. A Saboteur could cut the power supply. A psion with a helmet senses the psions in the tunnels before seeing them. ¶ The house in Fairbanks, Alaska. A detached house in a suburb of Fairbanks, where Caleb Mercer lives with his mother. The neighbours think the Mercers are a normal family. In his room, Caleb Mercer hears humming at night. Under his mattress lies a small, milky crystal that he found on the bank of the Chena River. The crystal muffles the humming. Caleb Mercer, his mother Sarah Mercer, who knows nothing about STILLWATER, and a STILLWATER team that watches the house around the clock are there. You get in through the front door if you have a good excuse. The house is not secured, but STILLWATER’s watchers are alert. ¶ The branch office in Boston. A small office on an industrial estate, officially a branch office of the cover agency. It houses the handler Raymond Cole, a radio room and an archive of reports on O.D.I.N. movements in North America. Raymond Cole, two technicians and STILLWATER’s Remote Viewer, when he is in Boston, work there. You get in the same way as into the main building in Washington, only it is smaller and less well secured. A Spy with a good legend gets in. A psion with a helmet senses the Remote Viewer before entering the building.

A decommissioned water reservoir in Maryland, about an hour from Washington. The tunnels are dry and have been used by STILLWATER since 1954. They house the Psionics Department of STILLWATER: a laboratory, a room with copper wire cages in which Psions without helmets are examined, and an archive with drawings by the Remote Viewer. Dr. Marcus Webb, two technicians and three psions who are no longer fit for duty work there. You get in through a locked maintenance shaft at the edge of the reservoir. The entrance is camouflaged and monitored by cameras. A Saboteur could cut the power supply. The neighbours consider the Mercers a normal family. In his room, Caleb Mercer hears humming at night. Under his mattress lies a small, milky crystal that he found on the bank of the Chena River. A psion with a helmet senses the Remote Viewer before entering the building.

- alt: Money: STILLWATER has an annual budget of about 40 million dollars, hidden in the budget of the cover agency. The money is enough for surveillance, travel, bribery and small operations. For large actions, Elaine Mercer has to go begging to the Department of Defense. ¶ People: About 80 people work directly for STILLWATER. In addition there are external contractors: Grey Meridian for armed operations, a dozen informants in Canada and the USA, two liaisons in the CIA who do not know what they are really working for. ¶ Equipment: The standard equipment of a US government programme: pistols, radios, surveillance technology, vehicles with disguised number plates. In addition, a small collection of crystals from finds in New Mexico and Alaska, about twenty pieces, most of them small and milky. No Alloy S. No helmets. ¶ Artefacts: The cap with copper wire from the seventies that the Remote Viewer wears. It is a stopgap that dampens the resonance, but does not stop it. STILLWATER has been searching for the Wardenclyffe Coil and the Porthole of the Eldridge for decades, without success. ¶ Connections: STILLWATER has access to the infrastructure of the US government: passports, visas, police reports, satellite images. The programme can monitor O.D.I.N. agents in North America without O.D.I.N. noticing. It also has a copy of parts of the 1989 list, which it holds back as leverage but has never used.

The programme can monitor O.D.I.N. agents in North America without O.D.I.N. noticing. It also has a copy of parts of the 1989 list, which it withholds as leverage but has never used.

### 05 STILLWATER › Key Figures

- alt: Elaine Mercer has been the Director of the programme since 2019. She pulled STILLWATER out of insignificance after its budget was almost cut after 2001. She is 58 years old, has grey hair and rimless glasses, and wears a dark blue blazer. She looks like the head of an agency who never sleeps. She wants a Seeker of her own, whatever it costs, and she wants to meet O.D.I.N. as an equal. She knows that O.D.I.N. has psions in large numbers, that the parts of the 1989 list held by STILLWATER exist, and that STILLWATER has psions of its own who do not live long without a helmet. She suspects that O.D.I.N. finds the children with a Seeker, but does not know it. Her secret: she is Caleb Mercer’s aunt. She discovered the boy herself, on a family visit to Fairbanks, and reported him to the programme. She told her sister that Caleb is in a programme for gifted children. She speaks calmly, precisely, without haste. She never says more than necessary, and she lies without changing her voice.
- neu: Elaine Mercer has been the Director of the programme since 2019. She pulled STILLWATER out of obscurity after the budget was almost cut after 2001. She is 58 years old, has grey hair, rimless glasses, and wears a dark blue blazer. She looks like an agency head who never sleeps. She wants a Seeker of her own, whatever the cost, and she wants to meet O.D.I.N. She suspects that O.D.I.N. finds the children with a Seeker, but does not know it. Her secret: she is Caleb Mercer’s aunt. She discovered the boy herself, during a family visit in Fairbanks, and reported him to the programme. She told her sister that Caleb was in a support programme for gifted children. She speaks calmly, precisely, without haste. She never says more than necessary, and she lies without changing her voice.

- alt: Special Knows the parts of the 1989 list held by STILLWATER and can have any O.D.I.N. agent in North America burned at any time ¶ Weakness Her sister and her nephew. Caleb is her weak spot, and her sister knows nothing ¶ Behaviour Negotiates, threatens, withdraws when the price gets too high. She does not fight herself, except when Caleb is in danger
- neu: Special Knows the parts of the 1989 list that STILLWATER has, and can have an O.D.I.N. agent in North America burned at any time ¶ Weakness Her sister and her nephew. Caleb is her weak point, and her sister knows nothing ¶ Behaviour Negotiates, threatens, withdraws when the price becomes too high. She does not fight herself, except when Caleb is in danger

- alt: Dr. Marcus Webb heads STILLWATER’s Psionics Department. He found most of the late-awakened psions and looks after them. He is 61 years old, a gaunt man with a grey beard, corduroy trousers and a jumper with leather patches on the elbows. He looks like a university professor who has lost his way. He wants to open Caleb Mercer before the boy awakens on his own and comes to harm. He believes that STILLWATER can make the opening safer than O.D.I.N. He knows that the programme’s own psions do not live long without a helmet, that the cap with copper wire from the seventies is only a stopgap, that O.D.I.N. uses Alloy S, but not where it comes from, and that STILLWATER does not know how the opening works. His secret: in 1989 he took part of the list from Langley and burned it. He wanted to prevent STILLWATER from using the names against O.D.I.N. He has never told anyone. He speaks slowly, in a lecturing tone, with many subordinate clauses. He likes to explain, even when no one is listening.
- neu: Dr. Marcus Webb heads the Psionics Department of STILLWATER. He found most of the late-awakened psions and looks after them. He is 61 years old, a gaunt man with a grey beard, corduroy trousers and a jumper with leather patches on the elbows. He looks like a university professor who has lost his way. He wants to open Caleb Mercer before the boy awakens on his own and comes to harm. He believes that STILLWATER can make the opening safer than O.D.I.N. He knows that their own Psions do not live long without a helmet, that the cap with copper wire from the seventies is only a stopgap, that O.D.I.N. uses Alloy S but not where it comes from, and that STILLWATER does not know how the opening works. His secret: In 1989 he took a part of the list from Langley and burned it. He wanted to prevent STILLWATER from using the names against O.D.I.N. He has never told anyone. He speaks slowly, lecturing, with many subordinate clauses. He likes to explain, even when no one is listening.

- alt: Raymond Cole is the handler of STILLWATER’s Remote Viewer. He coordinates the surveillance of O.D.I.N. in North America. He is 44 years old, has close-cropped hair, and wears a suit and an earpiece. He looks like a Secret Service agent who has been on duty too long. He wants to drive the cell out of North America before it finds Caleb Mercer. He wants to push the plan through without O.D.I.N. intervening. He knows that O.D.I.N. maintains relays in Montréal and Boston, that the Remote Viewer can see the cell before it sees him, and that STILLWATER tolerates O.D.I.N. in North America as long as O.D.I.N. finds nothing there. His secret: he has a brother who works for the CIA and knows nothing about STILLWATER. For years Cole has sent him reports on “weather data” that were in truth observations of O.D.I.N. He speaks tersely, in a military manner, with long pauses. He says “Copy” instead of “Understood”.
- neu: Raymond Cole is the handler of the Remote Viewer of STILLWATER. He coordinates the observation of O.D.I.N. in North America. He is 44 years old, has short hair, wears a suit and an earpiece. He looks like a Secret Service agent who has been on duty too long. He wants to drive the cell out of North America before it finds Caleb Mercer. His secret: he has a brother who works for the CIA and knows nothing about STILLWATER. For years Cole sent him reports about “weather data” that were in truth observations of O.D.I.N. He speaks tersely, in a military manner, with long pauses. He says “Copy” instead of “Understood”.

- alt: STILLWATER’s Remote Viewer is the best psion of the American programme. He is 39 years old, a pale man in a motel room with drawn curtains, surrounded by drawings. He wears a cap with copper wire from the seventies. He sees places where he has never been, and he has had headaches for as long as he can remember. He wants the headaches to stop. He wants someone to help him before he dies. He knows that STILLWATER uses him to observe O.D.I.N., and that without a helmet he will not live much longer. He has no idea that something can hear him. His secret: in recent months he has begun to draw places he does not know: a silence beneath a Hold, a room with a child who hears humming. He does not know what it means. He speaks quietly, haltingly, with long pauses. He rarely speaks in full sentences.

He speaks quietly, haltingly, with long pauses. He rarely speaks in full sentences.

### 05 STILLWATER › Relationship to O.D.I.N.

- alt: What O.D.I.N. knows: That STILLWATER exists, that it has psions of its own, that it tolerates O.D.I.N. in North America as long as O.D.I.N. finds nothing there. O.D.I.N. does not know that STILLWATER has found a boy who hears humming. O.D.I.N. does not know that STILLWATER has parts of the 1989 list. ¶ What STILLWATER knows about O.D.I.N.: That O.D.I.N. has psions in large numbers. That O.D.I.N. uses Alloy S. That O.D.I.N. is only tolerated in North America. STILLWATER suspects that O.D.I.N. finds the children with a Seeker, but does not know it. STILLWATER does not know how the opening works, and it does not know that with every opening about every third child goes.

That O.D.I.N. is only tolerated in North America. STILLWATER suspects that O.D.I.N. finds the children with a Seeker, but does not know it. STILLWATER does not know how the opening works, and it does not know that at every opening about one in three children disappears.

- alt: A new life in America, with real papers, for the character and their family. STILLWATER can make any O.D.I.N. agent in North America disappear and give them a new identity that holds even against O.D.I.N.
- neu: A new life in America, with real papers, for the character and their family. STILLWATER can make any O.D.I.N. agent in North America disappear and give them a new identity that holds up even against O.D.I.N.

### 05 STILLWATER › Overview

- alt: STILLWATER is the American counterpart to O.D.I.N.: a programme that has been hidden in changing US agencies since 1954 and is trying, with less knowledge, fewer psions and no Seeker, to gain the same access to the Choir as the Europeans. It tolerates O.D.I.N. in North America as long as O.D.I.N. finds nothing there. Now STILLWATER has found a boy who hears humming. For the game master, STILLWATER is the rival that watches from afar until it can act itself.

It tolerates O.D.I.N. in North America as long as O.D.I.N. finds nothing there. Now STILLWATER has found a boy who hears humming. For the game master, STILLWATER is the rival that watches from afar until it can act itself.

- alt: Seat Washington, D.C., officially the “National Atmospheric Data Center”, Division of Historical Weather Data
- neu: Seat Washington, D.C., officially “National Atmospheric Data Center”, Department for Historical Weather Data

### 15 The Evening › The Plan: “The Great Stone”

- alt: A bidder is looking for a crystal as large as a person, and the Evening has promised to deliver it. The Curator follows a trail of estates, old drilling logs and men who have seen too much. The cell notices it through questions asked at trade fairs, through equipment that fits in no auction warehouse, and through an old man who sits in the front row at every auction and never bids.
- neu: A bidder is looking for a crystal as large as a person, and the Evening has promised to deliver it. The Curator follows a trail of estates, old drilling logs and men who have seen too much. The cell notices it from questions asked at fairs, from equipment that does not fit into any auction warehouse, and from an old man who sits in the front row at every auction and never bids.

- alt: ☠○○○○ The Curator searches in old catalogues, estates and rumours. In Bologna he speaks with a dealer from Trieste. Trail: An Antiquarian hears at a trade fair in Bologna that someone is asking about a “very large piece”. ¶ ☠☠○○○ In Dresden the bidder buys at auction the estate of a geologist. In it lies half a page about a drilling in the ice. Trail: A Grey File emerges. In the post office box in Vaduz lies a message for the Curator: “The estate is here. The trail is thin.” A Spy reads it before the courier comes. ¶ ☠☠☠○○ The Curator prepares the access. In Genoa he meets an old man who was present at a drilling on Spitsbergen in 1962. Trail: An Infiltrator sees crates in the warehouse that are not meant for an auction: ropes, ice screws, cold-weather gear, a Geiger counter. ¶ ☠☠☠☠○ The bidder knows that the stone lies in a glacier on Spitsbergen, where in 1962 a drilling crew found something clear in the ice and abandoned the drilling. The Curator puts together a team from Grey Meridian. Trail: A Red File emerges. In Vienna, Marchetti hears the sentence: “The glacier gives it up, if you know where.”
- neu: The curator searches old catalogues, estates and rumours. In Bologna he speaks with a dealer from Trieste. Sign: An Antiquarian hears at a fair in Bologna that someone is asking for a “very large piece”. ¶ ☠☠○○○ In Dresden the bidder buys at auction the estate of a geologist. In it lies half a page about a borehole in the ice. Trail: A Grey File emerges. In the post office box in Vaduz there is a message to the curator: “The estate is here. The trail is thin.” A Spy reads it before the courier comes. ¶ ☠☠☠○○ The Curator prepares the access. In Genoa he meets an old man who was present at a borehole on Spitsbergen in 1962. Trail: An Infiltrator sees crates in the warehouse that are not meant for an auction: ropes, ice screws, cold-weather gear, a Geiger counter. ¶ ☠☠☠☠○ The bidder knows that the stone lies in a glacier on Spitsbergen, where in 1962 a drilling crew found something clear in the ice and abandoned the borehole. The curator assembles a team from Grey Meridian. Trail: A Red File emerges. Marchetti hears the sentence in Vienna: “The glacier gives it up if you know where.”

- alt: The Key Figure is the bidder. Without him there is no commission, and the Curator drops the search as soon as nobody pays any more. If the bidder is exposed, detained or convinced that the stone will not give back what he has lost, the plan loses two skulls and does not roll after the next mission. The bidder is no soldier; he is an old man with bodyguards, and the question of whether to take his hope from him is harder than any fight. ¶ What lies in the ice remains open. It can be a Nexus crystal, larger than any O.D.I.N. knows. It can be a stone that is already full. It can be a person who has been standing in a crystal since 1962. The game master decides it when the cell stands in front of it, and not earlier.
- neu: The Key Figure is the bidder. Without him there is no commission, and the curator drops the search as soon as no one pays any more. If the bidder is exposed, detained or convinced that the stone will not give back what he has lost, the plan loses two skulls and does not roll after the next mission. The bidder is not a soldier; he is an old man with bodyguards, and the question of whether to take away his hope is harder than any fight. ¶ What lies in the ice remains open. It can be a Nexus crystal, larger than any O.D.I.N. knows. It can be a stone that is already full. It can be a person who has been standing in a crystal since 1962. The game master decides it when the cell stands before it, and not earlier.

- alt: The Evening has Knowledge 2 and rarely notices who is harming it. If the cell disrupts an auction, the Curator has it watched and sends a messenger with an offer: a piece from the catalogue, if the cell withdraws. If it refuses, he doubles the price. If it attacks, the Evening breaks off the auction and is in another city the next morning.

If the cell disrupts an auction, the curator has them watched and sends a messenger with an offer: a piece from the catalogue if the cell withdraws. If they refuse, he doubles the price. If they attack, the Evening breaks off the auction and is in another city the next morning.

- alt: At ☠☠☠○○ : “The Chimera” (Morrow Biologics) loses a skull. The Evening ties up all couriers and channels for the stone and delivers no more samples to Morrow. ¶ At ☠☠☠○○ : “The Second Site” (Department 13) gains a skull. Through the Evening, the Department gets hold of details about O.D.I.N.’s alloy stocks. ¶ At ☠☠☠☠☠ : “The Page from 1871” (Lodge of the Silent Circle) gains a skull. In the excitement over the great stone, the Council leaves the Registry unwatched, and the Lodge gets closer to the page.

In the excitement over the great stone, the Council leaves the Registry unobserved, and the Lodge gets closer to the page.

### 15 The Evening › Bases and Means

- alt: The hotel changes with every auction. In recent years it was the Hotel “Palais Achatmühle” in Dresden, the “Hotel Cordula” in Vienna, the “Albergo Tre Campane” in Bologna and the “Hotel Lanterna Nuova” in Genoa. In the ballroom stand the auction table, rows of gilded chairs and a podium with a glass display case; at the doors stand men from Grey Meridian. You only get in with an invitation card whose code is checked at the entrance. An Infiltrator can forge the code (Sleight of Hand against Hard (3)), a Negotiator can appear as a bidder, a Spy can slip into the staff. ¶ The warehouse is never in the hotel, but in a hall or a cellar on the edge of town, rented through a shell company. Inside are crates, safes and lead containers, an examination table, a camera and a camp bed for Radek. Two to four men from Grey Meridian keep watch. The code at the gate changes after every auction. ¶ The private viewing takes place in a suite or a salon. There the Curator shows one or two pieces that are not in the catalogue, and only to bidders he knows.
- neu: The hotel changes with every auction. In recent years it has been the Hotel “Palais Achatmühle” in Dresden, the “Hotel Cordula” in Vienna, the “Albergo Tre Campane” in Bologna and the “Hotel Lanterna Nuova” in Genoa. In the ballroom stand the auction table, rows of gilded chairs and a podium with a glass display case; at the doors stand men from Grey Meridian. One can only enter with an invitation card whose code is checked at the entrance. An Infiltrator can forge the code (Security Systems against Hard (3)), a Negotiator can appear as a bidder, a Spy can infiltrate the staff. ¶ The Warehouse is never in the hotel, but in a hall or a cellar on the outskirts, rented through a shell company. In it stand crates, safes and lead containers, an inspection table, a camera and a camp bed for Radek. Two to four men from Grey Meridian stand guard. The code at the gate changes after every auction. ¶ The private viewing takes place in a suite or a salon. The curator shows one or two pieces there that are not in the catalogue, and only to bidders he knows.

- alt: Money: Every auction brings in two to five million euros in commission and buyer’s premium, through accounts in Vaduz, Luxembourg and Singapore. About eight million are available for purchases, bribery and security. ¶ People: The organiser, the Curator, the warehouse master and four to six permanent staff as drivers, examiners and couriers. In addition, for each auction, ten to twenty Grey Meridian operators and hotel staff who know nothing. ¶ Equipment: Two armoured vans, rented through a company in Dubai, invitation cards with one-time codes, a catalogue that is burned after the auction, and a lead container that once belonged to a Hold. ¶ Connections: Morrow Biologics buys, Grey Meridian protects, the Foundation in Basel bids along to take crystals off the market, and Department 13 occasionally delivers from Siberia.

About eight million are available for purchases, bribery and security. ¶ People: The organiser, the curator, the warehouse master and four to six permanent staff as drivers, inspectors and couriers. In addition, per auction ten to twenty operators from Grey Meridian and hotel staff who know nothing. ¶ Equipment: Two armoured transporters, rented through a company in Dubai, invitation cards with one-time codes, a catalogue that is burned after the auction, and a lead container that once belonged to a Hold. ¶ Connections: Morrow Biologics buys, Grey Meridian protects, the Foundation in Basel bids to take crystals off the market, and Department 13 occasionally delivers from Siberia.

### 15 The Evening › Overview

- alt: The Evening is not an organisation with a headquarters, but a market. Four times a year, in a different city, in a hotel with a ballroom, crystals are auctioned, artefacts, samples from rifts and sometimes people who have seen something. Whoever is invited receives a card without a sender. For the game master, the Evening is the place where the cell sees who all is searching behind the Veil, and what they are prepared to give for it. ¶ “We do not sell answers. We sell the questions others are not allowed to ask.”
- neu: The Evening is not an organisation with a headquarters, but a market. Four times a year, in a different city, in a hotel with a ballroom, crystals are auctioned, artefacts, samples from rifts and sometimes people who have seen something. Whoever is invited receives a card without a sender. For the game master, the Evening is the place where the cell sees who is searching behind the Veil, and what they are willing to give for it. ¶ “We do not sell answers. We sell the questions that others aren’t allowed to ask.”

### 15 The Evening › Hooks and Outlook

- alt: White File “The Card Without a Sender”. In a Viennese antiquarian bookshop an invitation card turns up, addressed to a man who has been dead for two years. The card is valid for an auction in Dresden, in three days. The dead man was once an agent of the Operations Branch. ¶ Grey File “The Catalogue”. A Spy among the hotel staff photographed a catalogue before it was burned. On page four is a small crystal with an abbreviation from the Crystal Ledger. The cell is to bring it back before it is auctioned in Genoa. ¶ Red File “The Man in the Front Row”. Marchetti has heard that the Bidder with the Great Stone has found a trail. The Council wants to know who he is. The cell is to slip into the auction in Vienna and identify him. He is there, and he recognises the cell first. ¶ Black File “The Glacier”. The bidder is on his way to Spitsbergen with a team from Grey Meridian. The cell is to stop him before he touches the stone, and nobody at the Council will say why the file is black.
- neu: White File “The Card Without a Sender”. In a Viennese antiquarian bookshop, an invitation card appears, addressed to a man who has been dead for two years. The card is valid for an auction in Dresden, in three days. The dead man was once an agent of the Operations Branch. ¶ Grey File “The Catalogue”. A Spy among the hotel staff photographed a catalogue before it was burned. On page four there is a small crystal with an abbreviation from the Crystal Ledger. The cell is to retrieve it before it is auctioned in Genoa. ¶ Red File “The Man in the Front Row”. Marchetti has heard that the bidder with the great stone has found a lead. The Council wants to know who he is. The cell is to slip into the auction in Vienna and identify him. He is there, and he recognises the cell first. ¶ Black File “The Glacier”. The bidder is on the way to Spitsbergen with a Grey Meridian team. The cell is to stop him before he touches the stone, and no one on the Council wants to say why the file is black.

- alt: If the bidder has the stone, a person outside O.D.I.N. owns something the Council did not know existed, and he will try to use it. If he has failed, the Evening has broken a promise for the first time, and in a world made only of trust and money, that is expensive. ¶ The next plan: “The Second Evening” ○○○○○ . The Curator founds a closed auction only for the bidders who wanted the great stone, and promises them to deliver next time what O.D.I.N. does not have.
- neu: If the bidder has the stone, a human outside O.D.I.N. possesses something the Council did not know existed, and he will try to use it. If he has failed, the Evening has broken a promise for the first time, and in a world that consists only of trust and money, that is expensive. ¶ The next plan: “The Second Evening” ○○○○○ . The curator establishes a private auction only for the bidders who wanted the great stone, and promises to deliver to them next time what O.D.I.N. does not have.

- alt: Whoever sits at the Evening is a hook in their own right. Roll a D6 for a bidder near the cell: 1 a biochemist from Morrow Biologics, 2 an art dealer from Geneva who buys for the Foundation, 3 a man with a Russian accent who never bids, 4 a widow looking for a stone in which her husband speaks, 5 a straw man bidding for the Lodge of the Silent Circle, 6 someone bidding for the Council who does not know the cell.

- alt: In File 2 “Find Site”, the plan loses a skull if the cell secures the stone and hands it over to the Collegium, because it does not reach the trade.
- neu: In File 2 “Find Site”, the plan loses a skull if the cell secures the stone and hands it over to the Collegium, because it does not enter the trade.

- alt: In the campaign, the bidder sits in the hall in Vienna in Mission 10 “The Evening” and says the great stone lies in a library that does not exist. He wants to fetch it on 14 February. Whoever plays the campaign follows it: the stone then does not lie in the ice, and the bidder’s knowledge of this place is the fifth skull.

### 15 The Evening › Key Figures

- alt: 58 years old, a severe bun, a dark blue suit that looks like a uniform. She controls invitation lists, cash flows and hotel contracts; without her, no auction takes place. She knows the most important bidders, the accounts in Vaduz and Singapore and the prices of the last twenty years. She knows that O.D.I.N. has people among the staff, but not which ones. Her secret: she was once a bookkeeper at a foundation in Zurich through which O.D.I.N. moved money, and she took files with her when she left. She speaks briefly and matter-of-factly and likes to end sentences with a sum.
- neu: 58 years old, a severe bun, a dark blue suit that looks like a uniform. She controls invitation lists, money flows and hotel contracts; without her no auction takes place. She knows the most important bidders, the accounts in Vaduz and Singapore and the prices of the last twenty years. She knows that O.D.I.N. has people among the staff, but not which ones. Her secret: She was once an accountant at a foundation in Zurich through which O.D.I.N. moved money, and she took files with her when she left. She speaks briefly and matter-of-factly and likes to end sentences with a sum.

- alt: In his mid-fifties, slim, grey hair, calm hands, a dark suit that looks one size too big. He runs the auction, examines the pieces and decides who is invited. He knows the origin and value of almost every artefact that crosses his table, and he knows that O.D.I.N. is watching him. His secret: he once sold a piece from an O.D.I.N. archive and afterwards let the buyer die, because he knew too much. He speaks quietly, with long pauses, and asks questions whose answers he already knows. Stats: Curator of the Evening (Threat Atlas).

He knows the origin and value of almost every artefact that crosses his table, and he knows that O.D.I.N. is watching him. His secret: He once sold a piece from an O.D.I.N. archive and then let the buyer die because he knew too much. He speaks quietly, with long pauses, and asks questions whose answers he already knows. Stats: Curator of the Evening (Threat Atlas).

- alt: 44 years old, tired, glasses, a worn coat; he looks like a lecturer who has not slept for too long. He is on the cell’s side, more or less. Every year he buys back a piece at the Evening that cannot be stolen. He knows the faces of the most important bidders and the names of three Spies among the staff. He knows that the Council sometimes buys at the Evening itself, and has stopped asking about it. His secret: he once offered a bidder a piece from an O.D.I.N. archive in order to get hold of another. The deal fell through, but the offer still stands.

The deal fell through, but the offer still stands.

- alt: Behaviour Observes, negotiates, withdraws. Only fights when there is no other way
- neu: Behaviour Observes, negotiates, withdraws. Fights only when there is no other way

- alt: An old man between sixty and seventy, very rich, very polite, with a face that gives nothing away. Who he is, the game master decides: Elias Vinter, a Danish shipowner who has made no public appearances since the death of his son in 2019, or Viktor Haller, a Viennese art dealer who signed over his entire property to a foundation in 2023. In both cases: he has lost a person who meant more to him than anything, and he believes that a large enough stone can bring that person back. He speaks slowly, with the patience of a man who has always been waited for.

In both cases: He lost a person who meant more to him than anything, and he believes that a sufficiently large stone can bring him back. He speaks slowly, with the patience of a person for whom one has always waited.

- alt: 38 years old, broad, shaved head, a scar over his left eye, leather jacket. He guards the pieces between arrival and auction. He knows the names of two O.D.I.N. Infiltrators and has not betrayed them yet, because he is waiting for a better offer. He once sold a piece from the warehouse; the Curator knows and uses it against him. He speaks briefly, suspiciously, and ends sentences with “and nothing else”.

### 15 The Evening › History and Goals

- alt: 1954 a closed auction takes place in Lisbon for the first time. An antiques dealer auctions three crystals from Anatolia to two private collectors and a man bidding for the Collegium. Because the auction only begins after dark, the dealer calls it “the Evening”. ¶ 1963 the Evening appears for the first time in a file of the Investigation Branch. An Antiquarian in Vienna reports an invitation card without a sender in the coat pocket of a dead art dealer. The file turns grey and is closed again: the dead man had debts, the killer was a creditor. The card stays in the archive. ¶ After 1971 the first samples from rifts appear at the Evening: a phial of condensation, a piece of stone that grows warm at night. ¶ 1989 the Evening auctions a piece of ashsilver from the workshop of Evan Pryce in Hamburg. For the first time, O.D.I.N. officially classifies the Evening as an opponent. The Operations Branch begins to smuggle personnel into the hotels.
- neu: 1954 a closed auction takes place in Lisbon for the first time. An antique dealer auctions three crystals from Anatolia to two private collectors and a man who bids for the Collegium. Because the auction only begins after nightfall, the dealer calls it “the Evening”. ¶ 1963 the Evening appears for the first time in a file of the Investigation Branch. An Antiquarian in Vienna reports an invitation card without a sender in the coat pocket of a dead art dealer. The file turns grey and is closed again: the dead man had debts, the perpetrator was a creditor. The card stays in the archive. ¶ After 1971 the first samples from rifts appear at the Evening: a phial of condensation water, a piece of stone that becomes warm at night. ¶ 1989 the Evening auctions a piece of ashsilver from Evan Pryce’s workshop at a sale in Hamburg. For the first time, O.D.I.N. officially classifies the Evening as an opponent. The Operations Branch begins to infiltrate personnel into the hotels.

- alt: 2024 a Spy in a Viennese hotel hears the warehouse master call the Curator “Mr Lindau”. Whether that is a name or an address, nobody knows. ¶ 2026 the Evening has a commission that is bigger than any auction: a bidder is looking for a crystal “as large as a person”. The Curator has promised to deliver it. ¶ What only the game master knows: The Curator does not know whether such a stone exists. He agreed because the bidder pays more than the Evening takes in ten years, and because a curator who says no soon has no bidders left. Since the spring he has been following a thin trail of old estates that leads north, into the ice.
- neu: 2024 a Spy in a hotel in Vienna hears the warehouse master call the Curator “Herr Lindau”. Whether that is a name or an address, nobody knows. ¶ 2026 the Evening has a commission that is larger than any auction: a bidder is looking for a crystal “as large as a person”. The Curator has promised to deliver it. ¶ What only the game master knows: The Curator does not know whether such a stone exists. He agreed because the bidder pays more than the Evening earns in ten years, and because a Curator who says no soon has no bidders left. Since spring, he has been following a thin trail of old estates that leads north, into the ice.

- alt: The Evening wants to trade. It wants to be the place where everything that is found behind the Veil gets its price. The organisers consider themselves neutral: they sell to anyone who pays and do not ask what happens to the pieces. They consider themselves more honest than O.D.I.N., because they hide nothing except their customers. That they sometimes auction people who have seen something, they call “witness protection for a bid”.

The organisers consider themselves neutral: they sell to anyone who pays and do not ask what happens to the pieces. They consider themselves more honest than O.D.I.N., because they hide nothing except their customers. ### 15 The Evening › Relationship to O.D.I.N.

- alt: What O.D.I.N. knows: That the Evening has existed since at least 1963, what it auctions and that the Curator is called “Mr Lindau”. Since this year, also that a bidder is looking for a stone “as large as a person”. ¶ What the Evening knows about O.D.I.N.: That O.D.I.N. is listening in and sometimes bids along through straw men. That there are archives whose contents would be worth millions on the market. The Evening does not want to know more; knowledge is bad for business.
