Since this year, also that a bidder is looking for a stone “as large as a person”. ¶ What the Evening knows about O.D.I.N.: That O.D.I.N. reads along and sometimes bids through straw men. That there are archives whose contents would be worth millions on the market. The Evening does not want to know more; knowledge is bad for business.

- alt: In return, the Evening wants a piece from an O.D.I.N. archive.
- neu: In return, the Evening wants a piece from an archive of O.D.I.N.

### 16 Grey Meridian › Key Figures

- alt: 61 years old, broad shoulders, short grey hair, a nose broken twice, expensive suits that never quite fit. He knows that his company guards things that do not belong in the world, and does not want to know more precisely. He is not an enemy of O.D.I.N., but a businessman with a business that is going too well to question. His daughter studies medicine in Edinburgh and does not know what her father does.
- neu: 61 years old, broad shoulders, short grey hair, a nose broken twice, expensive suits that never fit properly. He knows that his company guards things that do not belong in the world, and does not want to know more precisely. He is not an enemy of O.D.I.N., but a businessman with a business that runs too well to question. His daughter studies medicine in Edinburgh and does not know what her father does.

- alt: 44 years old, the man who carried the humming crate in 2014. He chooses who may escort the grey cargo. He knows that the crates are sometimes warm, sometimes hum and smell of ozone, and that operators who ask too much are transferred. He is loyal, but he has started taking notes. They are in a safe deposit box in London, under his mother’s name. Stats: Operator of Grey Meridian, subtype Team leader (Threat Atlas).
- neu: 44 years old, the man who carried the humming crate in 2014. He chooses who may escort the grey cargo. He knows that the crates are sometimes warm, sometimes hum and smell of ozone, and that operators who ask too much are transferred. He is loyal, but he has started to make notes. They lie in a safe deposit box in London, under his mother’s name. Stats: Operator of Grey Meridian, subtype Team leader (Threat Atlas).

- alt: 39 years old, now security consultant for a shipping company in Marseille. In 2019 in Antwerp she heard the rhythm, resigned and did not forget. She drinks too much, moves often, and her old dog growls at night when something that is not there passes the door. She does not talk willingly. But she wants to know what was in the crates, and whoever gives her an honest answer gets an honest statement.
- neu: 39 years old, now a security consultant for a shipping company in Marseille. In 2019 in Antwerp she heard the rhythm, resigned and did not forget. She drinks too much, moves often, and her old dog growls at night when something passes the door that is not there. She does not talk willingly. But she wants to know what was in the crates, and whoever gives her an honest answer gets an honest statement.

- alt: 34 years old, one of the company’s youngest operations managers. In 2017 in Dubai he guarded a crate that whispered at night, like a child counting. For two years he searched for what he had been guarding, and in the end he found a name. So he applied to an address from an old newspaper. He does not know that his letter arrived. He carries a notebook containing everything he knows about the grey cargo, and he is afraid that someone will find it, and even more afraid that no one will ever read it.
- neu: 34 years old, one of the youngest operations managers in the company. In 2017 he guarded a crate in Dubai that whispered at night, like a child counting. For two years he searched for what he had guarded there, and in the end found a name. So he applied to an address from an old newspaper. He does not know that his letter has arrived. He carries a notebook with him in which everything he knows about the grey freight is written, and he is afraid that someone will find it, and even more afraid that no one will ever read it.

### 16 Grey Meridian › Overview

- alt: Grey Meridian is a private security company based in London, with offices in Dubai and Johannesburg. It supplies the mercenaries described in the Core Rulebook: well armed, well trained, badly informed. It works for anyone who pays, for the Evening, for Morrow Biologics and, in 2019, through three shell companies, even for the Council. For the game master, Grey Meridian is the team standing on the other side of the underground car park, and sometimes the only adversary you can talk to.
- neu: Grey Meridian is a private security company based in London, with offices in Dubai and Johannesburg. It supplies the mercenaries described in the Core Rulebook: well armed, well trained, badly informed. It works for anyone who pays, for the Evening, for Morrow Biologics and, in 2019, through three shell companies, even for the Council. For the game master, Grey Meridian is the team on the other side of the underground car park, and sometimes the only opponent one can talk to.

### 16 Grey Meridian › Bases and Means

- alt: London. One floor of an office tower in Canary Wharf, officially a management consultancy. In the basement stands the server with the assignments of the last ten years, encrypted, but not well enough for a Cryptanalyst: Cryptography against Hard (3). The problem is getting in. ¶ Dubai. The office for the Middle East, Central Asia and East Africa, smaller than London and better guarded. In a warehouse at the port lies equipment that would not be permitted in Europe. ¶ Johannesburg. Naidoo’s office. Here Grey Meridian also works as a procurer for collectors. In the vault lie maps of mines in which something was found that is not gold. ¶ On the move. No barracks. The operators live in rented flats, receive their assignments encrypted and drive rental cars that go back after every operation. Only the weapons belong to the company; their depots change every six months.
- neu: London. One floor in an office tower in Canary Wharf, officially a management consultancy. In the basement is the server with the contracts of the last ten years, encrypted, but not good enough for a Cryptanalyst: Cryptography against Hard (3). The problem is getting in. ¶ Dubai. The office for the Middle East, Central Asia and East Africa, smaller than London and better guarded. In a warehouse at the harbour lies equipment that would not be permitted in Europe. ¶ Johannesburg. Naidoo’s office. Here Grey Meridian also works as a procurer for collectors. In the safe are maps of mines where something was found that is not gold. ¶ On the move. No barracks. The operators live in rented flats, receive their contracts encrypted and drive hire cars that are returned after every operation. Only the weapons belong to the company; their depots change every six months.

- alt: Grey Meridian has no psions, no thaumaturges and no artefacts. The company has money, weapons and about 340 permanent and 900 freelance people, former soldiers and police officers from a dozen countries. A team is four to six operators and a team leader, plus a technician or a dog handler if needed; all stats are in the Threat Atlas under “Operator of Grey Meridian”. The teams have carbines, protective vests, encrypted radio, night vision and drones, but no incendiary ammunition, no silver and no Alloy S. In 2016 Callum Hart wrote an internal rule: “If the target does not react to lead, the assignment is over.”

Callum Hart wrote an internal rule in 2016: “If the target does not react to lead, the contract is over.”

### 16 Grey Meridian › Relationship to O.D.I.N.

- alt: For O.D.I.N., Grey Meridian is not an enemy but a tool that others use too. The cell can bribe a team, intimidate it, take it out or outbid its client. That is the company’s weakness: it is not fanatical, not even convinced. It is only expensive. The Council paid it itself and keeps quiet about it; the Audit would like to know why.

### 16 Grey Meridian › Who Is Paying Right Now

- alt: If the cell runs into a team or wants to know what the company is doing right now, the game master rolls a D6.

The box is sealed, questions are forbidden ¶ 2 Morrow Biologics Guard a laboratory near Lyon where at night something knocks on the doors It is about animal testing. One of the operators is afraid ¶ 3 A collector from Zurich Secure a villa on Lake Geneva with a warm stone in its cellar The team leader saw the collector touch the stone, and what he looked like afterwards

### 16 Grey Meridian › The Applicant

- alt: Sam Ferris is a man who knows too much and understands too little. The cell can recruit him by giving him an honest answer; then it has a level 2 contact at Grey Meridian who supplies operational plans and names and warns when an assignment smells of grey cargo. He is not an agent but an informant with fear: whoever uses him too often gets him exposed, and Grey Meridian makes him disappear. If the cell lies to him, threatens or ignores him, he becomes a problem. He knows that O.D.I.N. exists, and he could go to someone who pays more: STILLWATER or Department 13.
- neu: Sam Ferris is a man who knows too much and understands too little. The cell can recruit him by giving him an honest answer; then it has a level 2 contact at Grey Meridian who provides deployment plans and names and warns when a contract smells of grey freight. He is not an agent, but a frightened informant: whoever uses him too often gets him exposed, and Grey Meridian makes him disappear. If the cell lies to him, threatens him, or ignores him, he becomes a problem. He knows that O.D.I.N. exists, and he could go to someone who pays more: STILLWATER or Department 13.

### 16 Grey Meridian › Hooks

- alt: Grey File “Grey Cargo”. An operator has disappeared in Rotterdam after carrying a crate that hummed. His sister found an address in his papers and wrote a letter that reached O.D.I.N. The trail leads to a warehouse, to a team leader who will not talk, and to a crate that is still there. ¶ Grey File “The Applicant”. The Registry asks the cell to observe an operations manager of Grey Meridian who has applied. Only observe. On the third day he notices them. ¶ Red File “The Rhythm”. A former operator who was there in Antwerp in 2019 lies in a London clinic and talks of crates that hum in rhythm. A journalist has picked up on the story. The cell is to calm the man, distract the journalist and find out what the Council had guarded in Antwerp in 2019. The trail leads to Marlene Duval and to Naidoo’s safe.

The trail leads to a warehouse, to a team leader who will not talk, and to a crate that is still there. ¶ Grey File “The Applicant”. The Registry asks the cell to observe a Grey Meridian operations manager who has applied. Only observe. On the third day he notices them. ¶ Red File “The Rhythm”. The cell is to calm the man, distract the journalist and find out what the Council had guarded in Antwerp in 2019. The trail leads to Marlene Duval and to Naidoo’s safe.

### 16 Grey Meridian › History

- alt: 2005 Duncan Reeve and Priya Naidoo found the company in London. Reeve was an officer in the Parachute Regiment and afterwards in the private security business in the Balkans; Naidoo, a lawyer from Johannesburg, brings money and contracts. The name comes from Reeve’s time as a navigation instructor: the prime meridian, from which the world is measured. Grey, because nothing the company does is black or white. ¶ Until 2013 Grey Meridian protects cargo ships, oil rigs and corporate headquarters and grows, because it delivers two things: discretion, and teams that still function when the assignment is not in the paperwork. Dubai is added in 2008, Johannesburg in 2011. ¶ 2014 a team in Rotterdam collects a crate from a warehouse, the client a letterbox company from Geneva. The crate hums. Two operators refuse to touch it. The team leader Callum Hart carries it to the van anyway and that night dreams of a child calling his name. He writes a report. Reeve strikes it from the file, pays the two operators a bonus and promotes Hart. ¶ Since then Grey Meridian has carried out a dozen such assignments. Officially the company knows nothing about them. The operations managers call it “the grey cargo” and talk about it only in hotel bars. ¶ 2019 Grey Meridian works for the Council through three shell companies: a warehouse in Antwerp has to be guarded, in which crates from the Congo are stored. The assignment runs to this day. The operations manager Marlene Duval reports that the crates hum “in rhythm, like a heartbeat”. The report is never passed on. Duval resigns six weeks later. ¶ What only the game master knows: The operations managers now know more than is good for them. One of them has applied to O.D.I.N., with a CV and a cover letter. One does not apply to O.D.I.N. The letter is in the Registry in Stockholm all the same, in a file that officially does not exist, and on the cover it says: “Observe.”

In 2008 Dubai is added, in 2011 Johannesburg. ¶ 2014 a team in Rotterdam retrieves a crate from a warehouse, the client a shell company from Geneva. The crate hums. Two operators refuse to touch it. The team leader Callum Hart carries it to the van anyway and dreams at night of a child calling his name. He writes a report. Reeve removes it from the file, pays the two operators a bonus and promotes Hart. ¶ Since then Grey Meridian has carried out a dozen such contracts. Officially the company knows nothing about it. The operations managers call it “the grey freight” and talk about it only in hotel bars. ¶ 2019 Grey Meridian works for the Council through three shell companies: a warehouse in Antwerp is to be guarded, containing crates from the Congo. The assignment runs to this day. The operations manager Marlene Duval reports that the crates hummed “in rhythm, like a heartbeat”. The report is never passed on. Duval resigns six weeks later. ¶ What only the game master knows: The operations managers now know more than is good for them. One of them has applied to O.D.I.N., with a CV and cover letter. One does not apply to O.D.I.N. The letter nevertheless lies in the Registry in Stockholm, in a file that officially does not exist, and on the cover it says: “Observe.”

### 16 Grey Meridian › Grey Meridian in the Plans of Others

- alt: Grey Meridian has no plan of its own, but the company makes the plans of others easier. If a team works for an organisation whose plan is currently running, then in the roll after the mission a skull is added to that plan one step earlier: at Strength 1 or 2 already on a 5 or 6, at Strength 3 already on a 4, 5 or 6, at Strength 4 or 5 already on a 3 to 6. This applies as long as the cell does not prevent the assignment. If it bribes the team, takes it out or gets the client to cancel the contract, the advantage is lost until the mission after next. ¶ Where a file gives Grey Meridian a skull, for example in File 3 “Number 341” (Grey Files), it goes to the plan the company is currently serving.

This applies as long as the cell does not prevent the contract. If it bribes the team, eliminates it, or gets the client to cancel the contract, the advantage is lost until the mission after next. ¶ Where a file gives Grey Meridian a skull, it goes to the plan the company is currently serving.

### 07 The Seventh Bureau › Overview

- alt: The Seventh Bureau is a name without an address. O.D.I.N. knows it from intercepted messages, from a file in the Registry and from the report of an agent who came back from Chengdu in 2016 and never wanted to go into the field again. Somewhere in China a unit predicts earthquakes, and at the places it names, rifts open strikingly often afterwards. This dossier gives the game master what O.D.I.N. knows, and three possibilities for what could be behind it. She chooses one of them or none.
- neu: The Seventh Bureau is a name without an address. O.D.I.N. knows it from intercepted reports, from a file in the Registry, and from the report of an agent who returned from Chengdu in 2016 and never wanted to work in the field again. Somewhere in China an agency predicts earthquakes, and at the places it names, rifts afterwards open with striking frequency. This dossier gives the game master what O.D.I.N. knows, and three possibilities for what might be behind it. They choose one of them or none.

### 07 The Seventh Bureau › The Agent of 2016

- alt: Martin Aebi, Swiss, born 1984, was an Investigator of the Investigation Branch with nine years of service in 2016. He spoke Mandarin and had specialised in East Asia, because there were blank spots there and he could not stand blank spots. In March 2016 he travelled to Chengdu as a Swiss geologist to follow up on the messages. The trip was approved as observation. Aebi was to look, not act. ¶ He came back in May, unharmed, and said almost nothing for three weeks. Then he asked for a transfer to the archive. The Branch refused, because Investigators with Mandarin are rare. Aebi repeated the request in writing, and a doctor at the Institute confirmed a stress reaction. Since 2017 he has worked in the Registry in Stockholm, with the maps that no one can classify. He does not talk about Chengdu. Whoever asks him gets a coffee and a question in return: “Have you written anything in chalk lately?”
- neu: Martin Aebi, Swiss, born 1984, was an Investigator of the Investigation Branch with nine years of service in 2016. He spoke Mandarin and had specialised in East Asia because there were blank spots there and he could not bear blank spots. In March 2016 he travelled to Chengdu as a Swiss geologist to follow up the reports. The trip was approved as observation. Aebi was to look, not act. ¶ He returned in May, uninjured, and said almost nothing for three weeks. Then he asked for a transfer to the archive. The Branch refused, because Investigators with Mandarin are rare. Aebi repeated the request in writing, and a doctor from the Institute confirmed a stress reaction. Since 2017 he has worked in the Registry in Stockholm, with the maps that no one can classify. He does not talk about Chengdu. Anyone who asks him gets a coffee and a question in return: “Have you written anything with chalk recently?”

- alt: 14 March. The university seismologist was polite, nervous and looked at his phone three times. When we parted he asked whether I was here because of “the Seventh”. I said I did not know what he meant. He smiled: “That is what everyone says.” ¶ 22 March. An old surveyor in the teahouse at People’s Park calls it “the bureau that asks the earth”. I asked how he knows. He pointed at the chessboard: “The knight does not know either, but he jumps.” ¶ 2 April. There is a First to Sixth Bureau in every list I find. There is no Seventh. But in a side street in the west of the city stands a house without a number, in which a light burns at night. The third time, a man in a grey suit stood in front of the door, barefoot, and looked at me as if he had been expecting me. He did not follow me. ¶ 9 April. In an alley behind the station, a number in chalk on the wall: 7. Beneath it a date, 20 April, and a place, Litang. I took a photograph. In the photo the wall is empty. The chalk on my fingers smelled of thunderstorms. I want to go to the archive. I want the files to be quiet.

I said I did not know what he meant. He smiled: “That is what everyone says.” ¶ 22 March. An old surveyor in the teahouse by the People’s Park calls it “the bureau that asks the earth”. I asked how he knows. He pointed at the chessboard: “The knight does not know either, but it jumps.” ¶ 2 April. There is a First to Sixth Bureau in every list I find. There is no Seventh. But in a side street in the west of the city stands a house without a number, in which a light burns at night. The third time, a man in a grey suit stood in front of the door, barefoot, and looked at me as if he had expected me. He did not follow me. ¶ 9 April. In an alley behind the station, a chalk number on the wall: 7. Underneath, a date, 20 April, and a place, Litang. I took a photograph. In the photo the wall is empty. The chalk on my fingers smelled of thunderstorms. I want to go to the archive. I want the files to be quiet.

### 07 The Seventh Bureau › In Play

- alt: The Seventh Bureau is a blank spot, and it may stay that way. A group can play for years without ever coming to China, and File 2022-031 remains a shadow on the Registry’s shelf. Whoever uses the Bureau should do so slowly: first a name in a report, then a crystal at the Evening, then an envelope at the seventh place setting. The cell should never fully understand the Bureau, not even at the end of a plan.
- neu: The Seventh Bureau is a blank spot, and it must stay that way. A group can play for years without ever going to China, and File 2022-031 remains a shadow on the shelf of the Registry. Whoever uses the Bureau should do it slowly: first a name in a report, then a crystal at the Evening, then an envelope at the seventh place setting. The cell should never fully understand the Bureau, not even at the end of a plan.

- alt: Martin Aebi is the way in. He knows more than is in his report. Whoever visits him in Stockholm and gives him time can get him to talk about the house without a number: Insight against Hard (3) to recognise that he wants to talk, and Persuasion against Very Hard (4) so that he does. What he then tells depends on the truth the game master has chosen. Only one thing he tells in every one: that after the chalk number in the alley, someone who wore no shoes followed him every evening for two weeks, and that on the flight to Zurich he did not dare to fall asleep.

Anyone who visits him in Stockholm and gives him time can get him to talk about the house without a number: Insight against Hard (3) to recognise that he wants to talk, and Persuasion against Very Hard (4) so that he does it. What he then tells depends on the truth the game master has chosen. Only one thing he tells in every version: that after the chalk number in the alley, someone followed him every evening for two weeks, someone who wore no shoes, and that on the flight to Zurich he did not dare fall asleep.

### 07 The Seventh Bureau › What O.D.I.N. Knows

- alt: Between 2011 and 2014, Cryptanalysts of the Investigation Branch in Hong Kong, as a by-catch of an entirely different surveillance operation, intercepted encrypted messages from a unit that called itself the “Seventh Bureau”. They were only analysed in 2015. At irregular intervals the unit reported coordinates to a recipient in Beijing, places in Sichuan, Yunnan and in the west of Hubei. A few weeks after each message the earth shook at that place, exactly on the predicted day. ¶ That alone would be remarkable, but not O.D.I.N.’s business. What caught the Registry’s attention: at at least three of these places, a rift opened after the earthquake. One of them, in March 2022 in the mountains near Kangding, was reported by a local informant before anyone could close it. It was barely two metres long, and it hummed. ¶ The Registry keeps File 2022-031, classification Grey. It holds the messages, the coordinates, the earthquake data and a note: “Origin unknown. Relationship unknown. Strength unknown. Knowledge unknown. Plan unknown.” In 2023 the Registrar wrote beneath it in pencil: “Do not touch until we know more. Whoever predicts earthquakes by predicting rifts knows more than we do.” ¶ Officially, that is all. China is a blank spot for O.D.I.N. There is no Hold there, no cell and only a handful of informants who do not know whom they work for.

They were only analysed in 2015. At irregular intervals the unit reported coordinates to a recipient in Beijing, places in Sichuan, Yunnan and in the west of Hubei. A few weeks after each report, the earth shook at that place, exactly on the predicted day. ¶ That alone would be remarkable, but not O.D.I.N.’s business. What made the Registry take notice: in at least three of these places, a rift opened after the earthquake. One of them, in March 2022 in the mountains near Kangding, was reported by a local informant before anyone could close it. Whoever predicts earthquakes by predicting rifts knows more than we do.” ¶ Officially, that is all. China is a blank spot for O.D.I.N. There is no Hold there, no cell, and only a handful of informants who do not know for whom they work.

### 07 The Seventh Bureau › Three Possible Truths

- alt: The game master chooses one truth for her group. The other two then do not apply, not even as rumour. Each truth brings Strength, Knowledge, a plan and a hook. All names, units and places in them are invented. The Seventh Bureau appears in no real organisational chart, and no real authority is meant.
- neu: The game master chooses one truth for their group. The other two then do not apply, not even as a rumour. Each truth brings Strength, Knowledge, a plan and a hook. All names, offices and places in them are invented. The Seventh Bureau appears in no real organisational chart, and no real authority is meant.

- alt: Relationship unknown · Strength 3 · Knowledge 4 · Plan: “Asking the Earth” ☠○○○○ ¶ The Seventh Bureau is a research unit, founded in the seventies after a rift from which a voice spoke was found during the clean-up after an earthquake. Since then the unit has learned to predict rifts by listening to frequencies that O.D.I.N.’s rift detectors do not know. It does not know what lies behind the rifts. It wants to know before it comes out by itself. It does not know that O.D.I.N. exists, but it suspects that it is not alone. Its head is Dr. Lin Shuyan, in her early fifties, head of frequency analysis, who has measured rifts for thirty years and never entered one. She is the Key Figure. She is not afraid of the answer.

- alt: ☠○○○○ New measuring stations at predicted places. A journal that is also read in Bern prints a study on “anomalous precursors”. ¶ ☠☠○○○ In the mountains of Sichuan, an apparatus, the Resonator, holds a small rift open. A herdsman reports a hum that grows louder at night. ¶ ☠☠☠○○ A measuring probe goes through the rift and transmits for 47 seconds. In a dead letter box in Hong Kong lies a measurement log that someone wants to pass to O.D.I.N. ¶ ☠☠☠☠○ The unit realises that the frequencies of the rifts resemble those of children singing in their sleep. It collects sound recordings from children’s hospitals and plays them to the Resonator.

In a dead drop in Hong Kong lies a measurement log that someone wants to pass to O.D.I.N. ¶ ☠☠☠☠○ The unit realises that the frequencies of the rifts resemble those of children singing in their sleep. - alt: Hook. A retired Antiquarian sends the Registry a Chinese study that he does not understand. The series of measurements in it follows exactly the pattern of the rifts that O.D.I.N. knows. The cell is to find out who wrote it. The author is giving a lecture in Geneva in the autumn.
- neu: Hook. A retired Antiquarian sends the Registry a Chinese study that he does not understand. The measurement series in it follows exactly the pattern of the rifts that O.D.I.N. The cell is to find out who wrote it. The author is giving a lecture in Geneva in the autumn.

- alt: Relationship unknown · Strength 4 · Knowledge 5 · Plan: “Closing the Doors” ☠☠○○○ ¶ The Seventh Bureau is older than any state in which it has hidden. Under changing names it has administered the rifts in China for centuries: it records them, seals them and guards them with families who pass the office from father to daughter. It knows what rifts are. It knows that they are doors and that someone waits behind them. It also knows that in the West someone sometimes opens the doors, and it considers O.D.I.N. children playing with fire. Its head is the Administrator, an old man without a name in the house without a number, who goes barefoot because he wants to feel the earth. He is the Key Figure. He speaks rarely, and then in the language of old files.

- alt: ☠☠☠☠☠ The Bureau closes a rift that O.D.I.N. is watching, with a method that O.D.I.N. does not know. On the satellite image, grass grows there. The Registrar writes: “They can do more than we can.” ¶ Hook. An Antiquarian of the cell finds a Chinese sheet from the 18th century in an estate in Geneva. It describes a rift and a unit that “administers” it, and bears a sign: a circle with a line. The same sign appears on a current satellite photo, in chalk, on a rock near Kangding.

It describes a rift and an office that “administers” it, and bears a sign: a circle with a line. The same sign stands on a current satellite photograph, in chalk, on a rock near Kangding.

- alt: The Seventh Bureau is not an authority. It is a business that dresses as an authority, because no one asks an authority about its customers. Its founder discovered that rifts leave something behind if one is there at the right time: shards, crystals, sometimes more. The Bureau predicts the rifts in order to be there first, and sells what it finds through intermediaries to collectors all over the world. It does not know what rifts are, and it does not want to know. Its founder is Ms Chen Meilan, a former geologist who in 2008, after an earthquake in the mountains of Sichuan, saw a rift from which a crystal fell. She wears it on a chain. She is the Key Figure. She believes the earth wants to give her a gift.

She believes the earth wants to give her a gift.

- alt: ☠○○○○ A buyer for the Bureau acquires at auction at the Evening an old map with rift coordinates that O.D.I.N. lost years ago.
- neu: ☠○○○○ A buyer of the Bureau acquires at the Evening an old map with rift coordinates that O.D.I.N. lost years ago.

- alt: ☠☠☠○○ Through shell companies the Bureau buys pumps, generators and crystal shards. A bill of lading from Rotterdam to Chengdu is too heavy for “geological instruments”. ¶ ☠☠☠☠○ The Bureau holds the rift open and harvests. The yield is greater than expected. In the mountains, lights go out at night, and a herdsman finds birds that smell of thunderstorms. ¶ ☠☠☠☠☠ The rift answers the harvest. It opens wider than the Bureau wanted, and something comes through. An earthquake that no one predicted. ¶ Hook. At the Evening a crystal turns up that comes from a rift in China. The Curator discovers a signature he does not know, and offers O.D.I.N. the name of the seller, in exchange for a favour.

The Curator discovers a signature that he does not know, and offers O.D.I.N. the name of the seller, in return for a favour.

### 02 Morrow Biologics › Overview

- alt: Morrow Biologics is a biotech corporation based in Leiden, with laboratories in three countries. Officially, the company researches regenerative medicine. In truth, it considers cells from rifts a scientific discovery and cultivates them without understanding what it is dealing with. For the game master, Morrow is the most important source for the origin of the Transformed: chimeras, growths, people who are different after a clinical trial.
- neu: Morrow Biologics is a biotech corporation based in Leiden, with laboratories in three countries. Officially, the company researches regenerative medicine. In reality, it considers cells from rifts a scientific discovery and cultivates them without understanding what it is dealing with. For the game master, Morrow is the most important source for the origin of the Transformed: chimeras, growths, people who are different after a clinical trial.

### 02 Morrow Biologics › Relationship to O.D.I.N.

- alt: Who at O.D.I.N. deals with them. The Institute in Bern has been watching Morrow since the purchase of the beryllium licence. The Custodian at the cold store has a file on the rift samples that were sold through the Evening. The Operations Branch has recently had a spy in Morrow’s administration, who reports once a month. ¶ What O.D.I.N. knows. Morrow buys samples from the Evening, runs a risky trial and has hired former O.D.I.N. people. The file is thin, because Morrow was long considered harmless. ¶ What Morrow knows about O.D.I.N. Almost nothing. Dr. Holm knows the organisation from the inside, but keeps silent. The board considers O.D.I.N. a rumour or an authority that can be kept in check with lawyers.

Almost nothing. Dr. Holm knows the organisation from the inside, but remains silent. The board considers O.D.I.N. a rumour or an agency that can be kept in check with lawyers.

- alt: Healing. For an illness, an injury, a mutation. Morrow offers an agent a therapy that officially does not exist, and in return demands a sample from O.D.I.N.’s cold store.

### 02 Morrow Biologics › History and Goals

- alt: 2013. A researcher at Morrow Biologics, Dr. Joost Brandsma, buys a sample from a rift from the Evening. In it he finds cells that divide without telomere loss. The corporation launches an internal programme under the name “Project Endless”. ¶ 2015. Morrow Biologics acquires a licence for beryllium insulation from the sphere of O.D.I.N. and begins to research the technology backwards. The Institute in Bern registers the purchase but does nothing.
- neu: 2013. A researcher at Morrow Biologics, Dr. Joost Brandsma, buys a rift sample from the Evening. He finds cells in it that divide without telomere loss. The corporation starts an internal programme under the name “Project Endless”. ¶ 2015. Morrow Biologics acquires a licence for beryllium isolation from O.D.I.N.’s orbit and begins to reverse-engineer the technology. The Institute in Bern registers the purchase but does nothing.

- alt: 2018. The first Overgrowths appear. Morrow internally classifies the cases as “excessive regeneration” and moves affected patients to a private clinic in Leiden. The trial continues. Dr. Brandsma sends a sample to Morrow’s branch office in Basel and has it analysed there in secret. He never presents the results to the board.
- neu: 2018. The first Overgrowths emerge. Morrow internally classifies the cases as “excessive regeneration” and moves affected patients to a private clinic in Leiden. The trial continues. Dr. Brandsma sends a sample to Morrow’s branch office in Basel and has it secretly analysed there. He never presents the results to the board.

- alt: 2021. The laboratory in Lyon begins experiments to cross rift cells with animal tissue. The first hybrid dogs appear as a by-product of a programme for wound healing in dogs.
- neu: 2021. The laboratory in Lyon begins experiments to cross rift cells with animal tissue. The first hybrid dogs emerge as a by-product of a programme for wound healing in dogs.

- alt: 2026. The Chimera is unstable. In spring it breaks out of its cage and kills two researchers. Dr. Favre covers up the deaths as a laboratory accident and has the affected wing partially evacuated. The creature is recaptured, but it has divided. The laboratory is slowly losing control, but no one in Leiden wants to admit it. ¶ What only the game master knows. The cells from the rifts are not a discovery but a contact. They come from rifts and answer to the Voice. Whoever cultivates them is not cultivating tissue but a connection. Morrow considers the side effects technical problems that more research will solve. Dr. Brandsma has known since 2018 that the cells react to sound and has kept silent about it. Dr. Holm suspects the connection with the Voice and keeps silent out of fear.

Dr. Holm suspects the connection with the Voice and remains silent out of fear.

- alt: Morrow Biologics wants to understand the cells from the rifts and turn them into medicine. The corporation believes it has discovered a natural source of unlimited regeneration and wants to patent it, license it and sell it. The leadership in Leiden sees itself as the pioneer of a medical revolution. ¶ What Morrow does not understand: the cells are not a discovery but a contact. They come from rifts, and they answer to the Voice. Whoever cultivates them is not cultivating tissue but a connection. The corporation considers the side effects technical problems that more research will solve.

### 02 Morrow Biologics › Key Figures

- alt: A tall, pale man with rimless glasses and expensive suits, who speaks of groundbreaking therapies in supervisory board meetings. He wants to make Morrow the leading biotech corporation in Europe and believes that MB-7 is the key. He knows nothing about rifts, the Voice or O.D.I.N. His secret: in 2019 he personally stopped the internal investigation into the Leitners because he feared a scandal. He talks in figures and quarterly targets.
- neu: A tall, pale man with rimless glasses and expensive suits, who speaks of groundbreaking therapies in supervisory board meetings. He wants to make Morrow the leading biotech corporation in Europe and believes MB-7 is the key. He knows nothing about rifts, the Voice or O.D.I.N. His secret: in 2019 he personally stopped the internal investigation into the Leitners because he feared a scandal. He talks in figures and quarterly targets.

- alt: The researcher who found the cells in 2013. A Dutch biochemist with grey temples and tired eyes, who used to research stem cell therapies. He leads Project Endless in Leiden and firmly believes in the medical breakthrough. He knows that the samples come from the Evening, but considers that a legal grey area. His secret: in 2018 he sent a sample to Morrow’s branch office in Basel and hid the results from the board, because they showed that the cells react to sound. He speaks quietly, with long pauses, as if he were checking every sentence.
- neu: The researcher who found the cells in 2013. A Dutch biochemist with greying temples and tired eyes, who previously researched stem cell therapies. He leads Project Endless in Leiden and firmly believes in the medical breakthrough. He knows the samples come from the Evening, but considers that a legal grey area. His secret: In 2018 he sent a sample to Morrow’s branch in Basel and hid the results from the board because they showed the cells react to sound. He speaks quietly, with long pauses, as if checking every sentence.

- alt: Former biochemist of O.D.I.N., who went to Asklepios and has worked for Morrow since 2019. A slender Swede with close-cropped hair and a scar on the back of his left hand, which he covers with a watch. He leads the department for cell stability in Leiden. He knows the list of the twenty-eight and suspects that the cells from the rifts are connected with the Voice. He has told no one, because he does not know whom he can trust. His secret: in 2025 he received a blood sample from Arvid Lund, recognised nanites in it and asked back: “Where did you get this?” No answer ever came. He speaks tersely, precisely, with technical terms, and avoids eye contact.
- neu: Former O.D.I.N. biochemist who went to Asklepios and has worked at Morrow since 2019. A slender Swede with short hair and a scar on the back of his left hand, which he hides with a watch. He leads the department for cell stability in Leiden. He knows the list of the twenty-eight and suspects that the cells from the rifts are connected to the Voice. He has told no one, because he does not know whom he can trust. His secret: In 2025 he received a blood sample from Arvid Lund, recognised nanites in it, and asked back: “Where did you get that?” No answer ever came. He speaks tersely, precisely, using technical terms, and avoids eye contact.

- alt: Behaviour Observes, keeps silent, helps only when he is sure it is against the Voice
- neu: Behaviour Observes, remains silent, helps only when he is certain it is against the Voice

- alt: A small, energetic woman with dark hair and quick movements, who wears a lab coat and safety goggles in a sterile laboratory. She created the Chimera and sees it as her life’s work. She knows that the creature is dangerous, but she believes she can control it. Her secret: she has fed the Chimera with her own blood to strengthen the bond. She speaks quickly, with a French accent, and becomes loud when interrupted.

- alt: Behaviour Defends the laboratory, does not negotiate, calls security and hybrid dogs

- alt: A woman in a grey trouser suit with a tablet and an earpiece, who waits for uninvited guests in sterile foyers. She knows what happens in the laboratories and protects the corporation anyway, because she believes in the medicine. Her secret: in 2024 she destroyed evidence about the Chimera, because she believed the corporation would otherwise halt the research. She speaks calmly, in short sentences, and answers questions with questions.

### 02 Morrow Biologics › Hooks and Outlook

- alt: White File “Cold Chain”. A Morrow Biologics van has crashed near Lyon. The police find empty cool boxes and traces of blood that match no known animal species. The cell is to find out what was being transported before the press does. ¶ Grey File “Bite Wounds”. In a suburb of Lyon, three dogs with armour-like growths are found. A vet reports the case to a number he should not know. The trail leads to a fence that has been bitten through from the inside. ¶ Red File “The Mother”. Homeless people are disappearing in the canals of Lyon. A sewer worker reports a creature with too many legs that divides when you shoot at it. The cell has to go into the sewers before the city floods the canals. ¶ Black File “Chimera”. The Council has sealed off the laboratory in Lyon. The Chimera has escaped and divided. The cell is to find the creatures and secure the evidence before Morrow destroys it. Dr. Norwood offers her help if, in return, the cell protects the patients of the MB-7 trial.

A vet reports the case to a number he should not know. The trail leads to a fence that was bitten through from the inside. ¶ Red File “The Mother”. The cell is to find the creatures and secure the evidence before Morrow destroys it. Dr. Norwood offers her help if the cell in return protects the patients of the MB-7 study.

- alt: The Chimera has established itself in the canals of Lyon. Three creatures hunt there, a fourth has disappeared into the Rhône. Morrow Biologics faces ruin: the MB-7 trial has become public, the shares have collapsed, and the board is trying to shift the responsibility onto Dr. Favre. O.D.I.N. has sealed the laboratory, but the creatures have escaped. ¶ The next plan of Morrow Biologics is called “The Last Dose” , starting value ☠○○○○ . The corporation wants to retrieve the last ampoule of MB-7, which O.D.I.N. keeps in the cold store in Bern, because something different was in this batch than in the others.
- neu: The chimera has established itself in the sewers of Lyon. Three creatures hunt there, a fourth has disappeared in the Rhône. Morrow Biologics faces ruin: Study MB-7 has become public, shares have collapsed, and the board is trying to shift responsibility onto Dr. Favre. O.D.I.N. has sealed the laboratory, but the creatures have escaped. ¶ The next plan of Morrow Biologics is called “The Last Dose” , starting value ☠○○○○ . The corporation wants to retrieve the last ampoule of MB-7, which O.D.I.N. keeps in the cold store in Bern, because something in this batch was different from the others.

- alt: The Leitners’ flight from File 5 “Second Circle” touches this plan directly. If the cell secures the Leitners together with their measurement data and scanner, “The Chimera” loses a skull, because O.D.I.N. gets its hands on evidence against Morrow and the corporation is forced to slow down its research. If the Leitners escape with their knowledge, the plan gains a skull, because then Dr. Favre works under the pressure that at any time someone with internal documents could go public, and she accelerates the experiments in Lyon. If the cell manages to convince Eva Leitner that O.D.I.N. is securing the crystal and that her children will not be tested, she can later testify as a witness against Morrow. Her testimony alone is not enough to stop the corporation, but it turns a Grey File into a Red one.

If the Leitners escape with their knowledge, the plan gains a skull, because then Dr. Favre works under the pressure that at any time someone with internal documents could go public, and accelerates the experiments in Lyon. If the cell succeeds in convincing Eva Leitner that O.D.I.N. secures the crystal and her children will not be tested, she can later testify as a witness against Morrow. Her testimony alone is not enough to stop the corporation, but it turns a Grey File into a Red one.

### 02 Morrow Biologics › The Plan: “The Chimera”

- alt: Morrow Biologics wants to turn the cells from the rifts into a regenerative therapy. The corporation believes it is cultivating tissue. In truth, it is cultivating a connection that answers to the Voice. The cell notices it first in the animals: dogs with armour plates, growths that heal in hours, and a laboratory in Lyon that requests more security personnel than a research laboratory ever needs.
- neu: Morrow Biologics wants to turn the cells from the rifts into a regenerative therapy. The corporation believes it is growing tissue. In truth, it is growing a connection that answers to the Voice. The cell first notices it in the animals: dogs with armour plates, growths that heal in hours, and a laboratory in Lyon that requests more security personnel than a research laboratory ever needs.

- alt: ○○○○○ The Chimera exists only as a cell culture in Lyon. Dr. Favre is planning the crossing with animal tissue. ¶ ☠○○○○ The first hybrid dogs appear and are used as guard dogs. A dog bites an animal keeper; the dog’s growths visibly heal within hours. Trail: A rumour in the canteen of Hold-7: dogs are disappearing in Lyon. ¶ ☠☠○○○ The Chimera grows and becomes restless. The laboratory increases the sedation. A Grey Meridian security guard quits and talks in a bar about “something in the basement”. Trail: A Grey File emerges: “Bite Wounds”. A dead dog with armour plates is found in a canal. ¶ ☠☠☠○○ The Chimera breaks out of its cage and kills two researchers. Dr. Favre covers up the deaths as an accident. The creature is recaptured, but it has divided. Trail: A newspaper report about an “animal attack” in Lyon. A contact of the cell reports that Morrow has partially evacuated the affected wing. ¶ ☠☠☠☠○ The second Chimera tries to break out but is held down by the sedation. The hybrid dogs become uncontrollable. Dr. Norwood activates the emergency protocol and calls Grey Meridian. Trail: A Red File emerges: “The Mother”. Animal carcasses that are still moving are found in the canals of Lyon. ¶ ☠☠☠☠☠ The Chimera escapes from the laboratory in Lyon and divides again. Three creatures hunt in the canals, and a fourth reaches the Rhône. Morrow finally loses control. Trail: The Council takes over with a Black File. The city of Lyon reports a series of animal attacks, and O.D.I.N. seals off the sewers. ¶ The Key Figure is Dr. Camille Favre, the head of the laboratory in Lyon. She created the Chimera and fed it with her own blood to strengthen the bond. As long as she lives, she holds the research together and covers up every incident. If the cell takes her out, the plan loses two skulls, because no one can claim control over the creatures any more and the laboratory panics. In addition, the plan does not roll after the next mission, because Morrow needs time to understand Favre’s work.

The laboratory increases sedation. A Grey Meridian security man resigns and tells people in a bar about “something in the basement”. Trail: A Grey File emerges: “Bite Wounds”. The creature is captured, but it has divided. Trail: A newspaper report about an “animal attack” in Lyon. A contact of the cell reports that Morrow has partially evacuated the affected wing. ¶ ☠☠☠☠○ The second chimera tries to break out, but is held down by sedation. The hybrid dogs become uncontrollable. Dr. Norwood activates the emergency protocol and calls Grey Meridian. Trail: A Red File emerges: “The Mother”. Animal carcasses that are still moving are found in the canals of Lyon. ¶ ☠☠☠☠☠ The Chimera escapes from the laboratory in Lyon and divides again. Three creatures hunt in the sewers, and a fourth reaches the Rhône. Morrow finally loses control. Trail: The Council takes over with a Black File. The city of Lyon reports a series of animal attacks, and O.D.I.N. seals off the sewers. ¶ The Key Figure is Dr. Camille Favre, the head of the laboratory in Lyon. She created the Chimera and fed it with her own blood to strengthen the bond. As long as she lives, she holds the research together and covers up every incident. If the cell takes her out, the plan loses two skulls, because no one can claim control over the creatures any longer and the laboratory panics. In addition, the plan does not roll after the next mission, because Morrow needs time to understand Favre’s work.

- alt: Morrow Biologics has Knowledge 1 and does not answer with a countermove. The corporation reacts to the cell like a company that fears a scandal. Dr. Norwood has the cell shadowed and gathers information about their relatives. The board brings in lawyers and threatens lawsuits if the cell comes near the laboratories. Dr. Favre accelerates her experiments because she wants to prove that she remains in control.

Dr. Norwood has the cell shadowed and gathers information about their relatives. The board brings in lawyers and threatens lawsuits if the cell approaches the laboratories. Dr. Favre accelerates her experiments because she wants to prove that she retains control.

- alt: At ☠☠☠☠☠ : “The Staircase” (the Singers) gains a skull. What escaped from Lyon sings in the canals, and the Singers follow the song to a staircase beneath the city.

### 02 Morrow Biologics › Bases and Means

- alt: Headquarters in Leiden. A glass office complex on the edge of the city, with a car park, a canteen and a wing for research. The board, the administration and Dr. Brandsma’s department sit here. In the basement are the cold rooms with the rift samples, secured by biometric locks and a team from Grey Meridian. You get in with a company pass, a good suit and an invitation, or through the loading bay if you know the delivery schedule. ¶ Lyon laboratory. A former clinic building in the east of Lyon, surrounded by a fence with barbed wire. Dr. Favre works here with a team of twelve researchers. In the basement are the animal pens, the cool boxes and the room in which the Chimera is kept. Access is strictly controlled, but the ventilation shafts are old and poorly secured. An Infiltrator with Technology against Hard (3) gets through. ¶ Private clinic in Leiden. A small clinic in a suburb, officially for rehabilitation patients. The Overgrowths from the MB-7 trial lie here, sedated and isolated. The staff are intimidated, the patient files are kept in duplicate. You get in as a visitor if you know the name of a patient, or as a doctor if you wear a white coat and act with confidence. ¶ Branch office in Basel. An inconspicuous laboratory on an industrial estate on the edge of Basel, officially owned by a subsidiary. Morrow stores samples here that should not be in Leiden, and this is where Dr. Brandsma had his secret analysis carried out in 2018. The entrance is a steel door with a number code; the camera above it is a dummy. A Saboteur with Technology against Doable (2) opens it.

- alt: Money. Morrow Biologics has an annual turnover of 2.4 billion euros and a research department with 180 employees. The corporation pays Grey Meridian 40,000 euros a week to protect the laboratories. ¶ People. 2,200 employees worldwide, 180 of them in research. In addition, a security team from Grey Meridian in Leiden and Lyon, each of four to six operators with a team leader. ¶ Equipment. Standard laboratory technology, beryllium insulation containers, cold rooms, an animal pen with 14 hybrid dogs, a vehicle fleet with three refrigerated vans. ¶ Artefacts. The rift samples themselves, among them the MB-7 cell line and the tissue of the Chimera. Morrow owns no crystals, but the samples are contaminated and react to the Voice.

### 03 The Singers › Hooks and Outlook

- alt: White File “The Humming Man”. A woman in Hanover reports to the police that her husband has been humming a melody she does not know for three days, and that he gets up at night and stands in the hallway as if he were waiting for something. The police pass the case on to O.D.I.N. because the woman says her husband never used to hum. The cell is to find out whether it is a case for the Print Shop or something else. It is something else. ¶ Grey File “The Staircase”. In Rotterdam, a car has been standing on the third basement level of a car park for three months. The owner, Daniel Koslowski, has disappeared. His wife Miriam followed him and, the night before, called the police and said she had found him. The cell is to enter the car park, find the fourth basement level and find out what is being sung there. ¶ Red File “The Song in the Car Park”. In and around Rotterdam, fourteen people have disappeared in one week. All of them last spoke of a staircase. The cell is to find the staircase before the police do. The trail leads to the fourth basement level of the car park, to a staircase that is only visible when you are alone and hear the song. ¶ Black File “All the Staircases”. During the night the Seeker heard the song, in four places at once, and a fifth spot that is still silent. She reported it to the Council and has not spoken since. The cell receives a list of five places: the Brocken, Rotterdam, a raised bog in Ireland, a reservoir in the Alps and a fifth spot that is crossed out on the list. The cell is to find the fifth staircase before the Singers open it. The fifth staircase is in a cellar in Tallinn.

The cell is to find the fifth staircase before the Singers open it. The fifth staircase is in a cellar in Tallinn.

- alt: The world has changed: on the night the Singers sang at all five staircases at the same time, people all over Europe heard the song. Not many, but enough. They did not go. They only understood that there is a way. Since then they hum when they are alone, and they look at doors differently. O.D.I.N. has sealed the five staircases, but the song can no longer be captured. It is in the world, like an earworm you can no longer get rid of. ¶ The next plan: “The Sixth Staircase” ☠○○○○ . The Singers are searching for the sixth staircase, and they know that there is one O.D.I.N. does not know about. It lies in a disused underground station in Budapest, and it leads not downwards but inwards.

The Singers are looking for the sixth staircase, and they know there is one that O.D.I.N. does not know about. It lies in a disused underground station in Budapest, and it does not lead down, but inward.

### 03 The Singers › The Plan: “The Staircase”

- alt: The Singers are searching for staircases. Every staircase found is another place where the world is thin, and another point where they can sing. The cell notices it because people disappear who have previously heard a song, because doors appear in places that should not exist, and because the Preacher moves from city to city and leaves behind congregations that wait for the song.
- neu: The Singers are searching for staircases. Every staircase found is another place where the world is thin, and another point where they can sing. The cell notices it because people disappear who have heard a song before, because doors appear in places that should not exist, and because the Preacher moves from town to town, leaving behind congregations that wait for the song.

- alt: ☠○○○○ The Singers have found a second staircase: the car park in Rotterdam. The Preacher begins to send people there. Trail: A missing persons case in Rotterdam that is reported to the Print Shop. A car that has been standing on the third basement level for three months. ¶ ☠☠○○○ The Singers have found a third staircase: the raised bog in Ireland. Tarek Mansour has mapped it. Miriam Koslowski follows her husband’s trail to Rotterdam. Trail: A woman in Rotterdam asking about a car park that officially does not exist. A dead drop in Hanover containing a city map on which two staircases are marked. From the plan a Grey File emerges for the cell. ¶ ☠☠☠○○ The Singers have found a fourth staircase: the reservoir in the Alps. The Preacher travels through northern Germany and the Netherlands. Tarek Mansour betrays the staircase in Ireland to the cell, because he becomes afraid that the Singers no longer need him, and because he hopes that O.D.I.N. will protect him. Trail: A Psion of the cell hears the song in their sleep. A call from Tarek Mansour to the police, who pass him on to O.D.I.N. ¶ ☠☠☠☠○ The Singers have opened all four staircases. The people who hear the song leave in droves. O.D.I.N. finds empty flats, abandoned cars, phones with voice messages in which the song can be heard. Trail: A newspaper report about a “sect” that is making people disappear in northern Germany. A call from Miriam Koslowski to the police, saying she has found her husband. From the plan a Red File emerges. ¶ ☠☠☠☠☠ The Singers sing at all five staircases at the same time: the Brocken, Rotterdam, Ireland, the reservoir in the Alps and a cellar in Tallinn. The song can be heard all over Europe, quietly, like a hum in the walls. Whoever hears it knows that there is a way. Trail: The numbers station broadcasts the song. The Seeker hears it and, for the first time in years, says nothing. The Council takes over with a Black File. ¶ The Key Figure is the Preacher. He knows where the next staircase is, and he sends people there. If the cell takes him out, the plan loses two skulls and does not roll after the next mission. But the Preacher is not a fighter. He is a man who hums, and whoever listens to him understands why people go.

- alt: 1 to 2 A bait. The Singers send the cell someone who has heard the song and wants to talk. He is friendly, confused and knows nothing. But while the cell questions him, someone else goes to the staircase. The witness disappears as soon as the cell lets him go, and leaves a message: “You didn’t stop him. You listened to him.” ¶ 3 to 4 A name. The Singers know a name that is sung in the stairwell. They sing it. An agent hears his name at night, sung by a voice that sounds like a person he knows. The next morning there is a stone outside his door with a circle scratched into it. ¶ 5 to 6 A contact falls silent. A contact of the cell, an informant, an old friend, hears the song and goes. He leaves a note: “I have understood. Don’t come after me.” The cell loses a level 1 or 2 contact until it finds him or gives up.

Kantor hears the Singers and believes that the choir is growing. ¶ At ☠☠☠☠☠ : “No More Nexus Crystals” (the Foundation in Basel) loses a skull. On the night of the song, the Council seizes all the Nexus crystals it can get in order to seal the staircases.

On the night of the song, the Council seizes all Nexus crystals it can obtain to seal the staircases.

### 03 The Singers › Relationship to O.D.I.N.

- alt: What O.D.I.N. knows: That the Singers exist. That they stand on the Brocken. That they speak with one voice. That a cult has arisen around them. That people disappear who have heard a song. That there is more than one staircase. Not where the other staircases are. Not what lies at the end of the staircase. Not how to stop the song, except with helmets of Alloy S.
- neu: What O.D.I.N. knows: That the Singers exist. That they stand on the Brocken. That they speak with one voice. That a cult has formed around them. That people disappear who have heard a song. That there is more than one staircase. Not where the other staircases are. Not what lies at the end of the staircase. Not how to stop the song, except with helmets made of Alloy S.

- alt: The Singers offer nothing. They sing. An agent who hears the song understands what it means: that there is a way, that his life is not everything, that he can go. That is the bait. No money, no power, no truth. Only the song. What they want in return: that he goes. That he goes down the staircase and does not come back. Or that he comes back and sings.
- neu: The Singers offer nothing. They sing. An agent who hears the song understands what it means: that there is a way, that their life is not everything, that they can go. That is the bait. No money, no power, no truth. Only the song. What they want in return: that they go. That he goes down the staircase and does not come back. Or that they come back and sing.

### 03 The Singers › History and Goals

- alt: 1989. On the Brocken, 23 people disappear from a decommissioned listening station. The attendance list of 30 April 1989 includes 23 people, none of them ever reappears. One person bore the surname Wendt. O.D.I.N. classifies the case as unexplained until, in 2026, a cell enters the station and finds the staircase (Core Rulebook, Prologue “Walpurgis”). The people on the staircase are the 23 from 1989, but they are no longer the same. They went through a rift and returned as something else. They speak with one voice. Around them a cult has arisen, without a leader and without a book. ¶ 2011. The accumulation begins. The new Seeker hears more children, and some of the children hum a melody in their sleep that the Seeker does not know. O.D.I.N. has kept the Singers as a case of their own since 2011. What the Council does not know: that year the Singers began to search for staircases. Not only the Brocken. They have the song, and the song shows them where the world is thin. ¶ 2019. The Opener beneath the Institute knocks every four minutes. In the same period, people all over Europe contact missing persons offices, saying their relatives had “heard a song” and then left. The Print Shop rewrites the cases as people leaving sects and as life crises. The Singers get two skulls. ¶ 2026. The cult is here. People from all walks of life leave their lives one evening and go to the next staircase. Some come back as Singers. O.D.I.N. has a Grey File, a list of places where staircases are suspected, and a preacher who moves from city to city. ¶ What only the game master knows: The Singers are not enemies in the classic sense. They are people who went through a rift and returned as something else. They speak with one voice because they are no longer separate. The cult around them is not an organisation but a contagion: the song finds people who have always had the feeling that their life is not their own. The Singers fetch no one. They sing, and whoever hears the song goes of their own accord. What lies at the end of the staircase remains open. The Singers know it, but they do not say it. They only sing it.
- neu: 1989. On the Brocken, 23 people disappear from the listening station, which is decommissioned today. The attendance list of 30 April 1989 includes 23 persons, none ever reappears. One person bore the surname Wendt. O.D.I.N. classifies the incident as unexplained until 2026, when a cell enters the station and finds the stairs (Core Rulebook, prologue “Walpurgis”). The people on the stairs are the 23 from 1989, but they are no longer the same. They went through a rift and returned as something else. They speak with one voice. Around them a cult has arisen, without a leader and without a book. ¶ 2011. The accumulation begins. The new Seeker hears more children, and some of the children hum a melody in their sleep that the Seeker does not know. O.D.I.N. has kept the Singers as a separate case since 2011. What the Council does not know: the Singers began searching for staircases that year. Not only the Brocken. They have the song, and the song shows them where the world is thin. ¶ 2019. The Opener beneath the Institute knocks every four minutes. In the same period, people across Europe report to missing persons units that their relatives had “heard a song” and then left. The Print Shop rewrites the cases as cult exits and life crises. The Singers get two skulls. ¶ 2026. The cult is here. People from all walks of life leave their lives one evening and go to the nearest staircase. Some come back as Singers. O.D.I.N. has a Grey File, a list of places where staircases are suspected, and a Preacher who moves from town to town. ¶ What only the game master knows: The Singers are not enemies in the classical sense. They are people who have gone through a rift and returned as something else. They speak with one voice because they are no longer separate. The cult around them is not an organisation, but an infection: the song finds people who have always felt that their life is not their own. The Singers fetch no one. They sing, and whoever hears the song goes of their own accord. They know what lies at the end of the staircase. The Singers know it, but they do not say it. They only sing it.

- alt: The Singers want all staircases to be found. They believe that the world is thin and that there are places where you can pass through if you know the song. They believe that they are saving the people who hear the song, because they show them that there is a way. They regard O.D.I.N. as a guardian who locks the doors so that no one sees what is behind them. They have no interest in power, money or revenge. They have only the song. ¶ What they believe, why they are in the right: Every person who hears the song has always known it. The Singers invent nothing. They only remember. Whoever goes, goes willingly. Whoever comes back, comes back because they have seen something they cannot forget. The Singers are the proof that it goes on. They are not evil. They have simply already been on the other side and can no longer pretend it does not exist.

The Singers are the proof that it goes on. They are not evil. They have only already been on the other side and can no longer pretend that it does not exist.

### 03 The Singers › Bases and Means

- alt: The listening station on the Brocken. A grey concrete block with a dome of weathered plastic, on the summit next to the transmission mast. In the main room lies a circle of salt, neatly scattered, no more than three days old. Since the 2026 mission a second Nexus shard has lain in it, which the Singers placed in the circle after the first one was secured. Beneath the circle, if you hear the song, the floor becomes transparent and shows the staircase. No one is there, except when the Singers sing. Then they stand on the staircase. The door stood open; since the 2026 mission an O.D.I.N. padlock has hung on it, which has to be broken open. ¶ The car park in Rotterdam. On the fourth basement level, in the corner behind the ramp, a staircase leads down. It is only visible when you are alone and hear the song. No one is there. But the traces of Daniel Koslowski lead here: his car is still on the third basement level, the key in the ignition. You reach the fourth basement level with the lift, which only goes there if you press the button three times. The raised bog in Ireland. A raised bog in which the Ogham stones move a hand’s breadth each year (Game Master’s Book, restricted zones table). A staircase begins beneath one of the stones. Tarek Mansour found and mapped it, but only he knows which stone it is. No one is there. You have to find the stone that moved last. Beneath it is the staircase. ¶ The reservoir in the Alps. A reservoir beneath which lies a church tower whose bell can be heard at low water (Game Master’s Book, restricted zones table). In the church tower, beneath the bell, a staircase begins that leads downwards, further than the lake is deep. It is only accessible when the water is low enough. No one is there. But on the nights when the bell can be heard, people stand on the shore humming the song. At low water a door in the church tower leads inside, which only opens if you hum the song.

Beneath the circle, when you hear the song, the floor becomes transparent and shows the staircase. No one is there, except when the Singers sing. Then they stand on the staircase. The door stood open; since the 2026 operation, a padlock from O.D.I.N. hangs on it, which must be broken open. ¶ The car park in Rotterdam. On the fourth basement level, in the corner behind the ramp, a staircase leads down. It is only visible when you are alone and hear the song. No one is there. But Daniel Koslowski’s traces lead here: his car is still on the third basement level, the key is in it. You reach the fourth basement level with the lift, which only goes there if you press the button three times. The raised bog in Ireland. A raised bog in which the Ogham stones migrate by a hand’s breadth every year (Game Master’s Book, restricted zone table). A staircase begins beneath one of the stones. Tarek Mansour found and mapped it, but only he knows which stone it is. No one is there. You have to find the stone that moved last. Beneath it is the staircase. ¶ The reservoir in the Alps. In the church tower, beneath the bell, a staircase begins that leads downwards, farther than the lake is deep. It is only accessible when the water is low enough. No one is there. But on the nights when the bell can be heard, people stand on the shore humming the song. At low water a door in the church tower leads inside, which only opens if you hum the song.

- alt: Money: None. The cult needs no money. People who hear the song leave behind their accounts, their houses, their cars. The Preacher lives on what he is given. Tarek Mansour has savings, which are used up. Miriam Koslowski has the joint account, which she does not touch. ¶ People: The cult has no membership lists. It has people who have heard the song and have not yet gone. The number fluctuates. In every city the Preacher has been to, a dozen to a hundred people stay behind, waiting for the song. They are not fighters. They are a congregation. ¶ Equipment: No weapons. No vehicles. The Singers at the Brocken wear coats from the eighties. The Preacher has a notebook with the names of the people he has sent. Tarek Mansour has city maps and a compass that does not point north but to the nearest staircase. ¶ Artefacts: The Nexus shard in the salt circle on the Brocken, inactive as long as the Singers do not sing. It is the anchor of the choir. If it is destroyed, the Singers fall silent (Core Rulebook, “The Singers”). The Singers themselves are the greatest artefact: people who went through a rift and returned as something else. ¶ Connections: The cult has no connections to other organisations. But other organisations have connections to it. The Lodge of the Silent Circle knows the song from 1871 and preserves it. The Open Ones under Kantor know that the Singers exist and consider them proof that the choir is larger than O.D.I.N. believes. The Congregation of the Silent Word has reports of singing children that could lead to the Singers. And the Foundation in Basel has a Nexus shard that resembles the one on the Brocken.
- neu: Money: None. The cult needs no money. People who hear the song leave their accounts, their houses, their cars behind. The Preacher lives on what is given to him. Tarek Mansour has savings that are used up. Miriam Koslowski has the joint account, which she does not touch. ¶ People: The cult has no membership lists. It has people who have heard the song and have not yet gone. The number fluctuates. In every city where the Preacher has been, a dozen to a hundred people remain behind, waiting for the song. They are not fighters. They are a congregation. ¶ Equipment: No weapons. No vehicles. The Singers on the Brocken wear coats from the eighties. The Preacher has a notebook with the names of the people he has sent. Tarek Mansour has city maps and a compass that does not point north but to the nearest staircase. ¶ Artefacts: The Nexus shard in the salt circle on the Brocken, inactive as long as the Singers do not sing. It is the anchor of the choir. If it is destroyed, the Singers fall silent (Core Rulebook, “The Singers”). The Singers themselves are the greatest artefact: people who have gone through a rift and returned as something else. ¶ Connections: The cult has no connections to other organisations. But other organisations have connections to it. The Lodge of the Silent Circle knows the song from 1871 and preserves it. The Open Ones under Kantor know that the Singers exist and consider them proof that the choir is larger than O.D.I.N. believes. The Congregation of the Silent Word has reports about singing children that could lead to the Singers. And the Foundation in Basel has a Nexus shard that resembles the one on the Brocken.

### 03 The Singers › Overview

- alt: The Singers are a cult without a leader and without a book, grown up around the people on the staircase at the Brocken, which leads deeper than a mountain can be. They are not an organisation that can be smashed. They are a song that will not let go of you once you have heard it. The game master does not play them as conspirators, but as a wave that is coming.
- neu: The Singers are a cult without leader and without book, grown around the people on the staircase on the Brocken, which leads deeper than a mountain can be. The Twelve of Asklepios are not an organisation that can be stormed. They are a song that does not let go of you once you have heard it. The game master plays them not as conspirators, but as a wave that comes.

- alt: Type Cult without a leader and without a book, grown up around the people on the staircase at the Brocken, which leads deeper than a mountain can be. ¶ Seat None. The Singers at the Brocken are the core, the cult moves around them.

### 03 The Singers › Key Figures

- alt: The cult has no leader. The following figures are the places where you can get hold of it.
- neu: The cult has no leader. The following figures are the places where it can be grasped.

- alt: The Singers do not call him by his name, but by the one they have given him: “The One Who Goes Ahead”. In O.D.I.N.’s files he is listed as “Preacher of the Singers”. He moves from city to city and tells of the staircase, the song and what waits behind it. He is the only one who has heard the Singers and come back without becoming one of them. He wants as many people as possible to hear the song before it is too late. He believes that he is saving them. He knows where the next staircase is, how to open it, and that the Singers at the Brocken are not the only ones. His secret: he was on the Brocken in 1989 when the 23 disappeared. He did not go with them because he was afraid. Since then he has heard the song every night, and he knows that one day he will go. He preaches to convince himself. He speaks quietly, kindly, without pathos, and often hums in the middle of a sentence. He never says “I” when he speaks of the Singers, but “we”, although he is not one of them.

He did not go with them because he was afraid. Since then he has heard the song every night, and he knows that he will go eventually. He preaches to convince himself. He speaks quietly, kindly, without pathos, and often hums in the middle of a sentence. He never says “I” when he speaks of the Singers, but “we”, although he is not one of them.

- alt: Wendt is the Psion who stepped into the circle on the Brocken in 2026 in the prologue “Walpurgis”. His grandmother was on the attendance list of 1989. He is missing, not dead, and he can come back as a Singer. That is an option for the game master, not a fixed truth. If he comes back, he seeks out people who already hear the song and brings them to the staircase. He wants people who hear the song not to be lost. He believes that he accompanies them, not fetches them. He knows how to recognise a staircase before it is visible, and that there are several staircases in Europe. His secret: he saw something on the staircase that he cannot forget, and he sings because he believes that only the song makes it bearable. He speaks haltingly, as if he first had to translate every word for himself, and speaks of himself in the third person when he talks about the past: “The man he was.”

He is missing, not dead, and he can return as a Singer. That is an option for the game master, not a fixed truth. He speaks haltingly, as if he had to translate every word first, and speaks of himself in the third person when he talks about the past: “The man he was.”

- alt: Attack Touch 4 + 3, damage 2 strain, whoever is touched hears the song for one scene and acts with −1 die on all tests. Otherwise no weapon.
- neu: Attack Touch 4 + 3, damage 2 strain, anyone touched hears the song for one scene and acts with −1 die on all tests. Otherwise no weapon.

- alt: Behaviour He watches, waits, accompanies. He does not fight. If he is cornered, he sings, and whoever listens understands why he goes.
- neu: Behaviour He observes, waits, accompanies. He does not fight. If he is cornered, he sings, and whoever listens understands why he goes.

- alt: Her husband, Daniel Koslowski, heard the song three months ago and left. She wants to follow him. She is not a Singer, but she has started to hum the song without noticing. She wants to find her husband again, or go to where he is. She knows where her husband was last seen: in a car park in Rotterdam whose fourth basement level has only existed since 2011 (Game Master’s Book, restricted zones table). She has his last messages on her phone: ¶ Voice messages in which he talks about a staircase. Her secret: she heard the song before her husband left. She hummed it to him without knowing what it is. She is the reason he left. She talks fast, breathlessly, as if she had no time, and says “we” and means herself and her husband, although he is no longer there.
- neu: Her husband, Daniel Koslowski, heard the song three months ago and left. She wants to follow him. She is not a Singer, but she has started to hum the song without noticing.
