She knows where her husband was last seen: in a car park in Rotterdam whose fourth basement level has only existed since 2011 (Game Master’s Book, restricted zone table). She has his last messages on her phone: ¶ Voice messages in which he speaks of a staircase. Her secret: She already heard the song before her husband left. She hummed it to him without knowing what it is. She is the reason he left. She talks fast, breathlessly, as if she had no time, and says “we” and means herself and her husband, although he is no longer there.

- alt: Special She knows her husband’s voice messages and can play them. Whoever hears them hears the song in the background. ¶ Weakness She is not a fighter. She breaks down if someone proves to her that her husband is not coming back. ¶ Behaviour She follows every trail that leads to a staircase, and she is prepared to lie, steal and put herself in danger for it.
- neu: Special She knows her husband’s voice messages and can play them. Anyone who hears them hears the song in the background. ¶ Weakness She is not a fighter. She breaks down if someone proves to her that her husband will not come back. ¶ Behaviour She follows every lead that points to a staircase, and she is willing to lie, steal, and put herself in danger to do so.

- alt: A slight man with a rucksack full of city maps on which he marks staircases. He is a member of the cult who knows the ways to the staircases. He used to be a cartographer at the land survey office in Hanover. He noticed that old maps show staircases that are missing from new ones. He wants to find all staircases before O.D.I.N. seals them. He knows the location of three staircases: the Brocken, the Rotterdam car park, the raised bog in Ireland. He knows the signs by which you recognise a staircase: cold spots, a smell of ozone, clocks that run wrong. His secret: he found the staircase in the raised bog in Ireland and gave the Singers only the map, not the stone beneath which it begins, because he is afraid that they will then no longer need him. He speaks precisely, with numbers and coordinates, and says “the staircase” the way others say “work”.

He knows the location of three staircases: the Brocken, the Rotterdam car park, the raised bog in Ireland. He knows the signs by which a staircase can be recognised: cold spots, smell of ozone, clocks that run wrong. His secret: He found the staircase in the raised bog in Ireland and gave the Singers only the map, not the stone under which it begins, because he is afraid they will no longer need him. He speaks precisely, with numbers and coordinates, and says “the staircase” the way others say “work”.

- alt: Attack Pocket knife 2 + 2, damage 2.
- neu: Attack Penknife 2 + 2, damage 2.

- alt: People in coats from the eighties who stand on a staircase that leads deeper than a mountain can be. They are the core of the cult. They sing, and their song is what other people hear. They want all staircases to be found so that they can all sing at the same time. They know what lies at the end of the staircase. Their secret: they are the 23 from 1989, but they are no longer the same. They went through a rift and returned as something else. They speak with one voice. They do not speak. They sing. When they speak, it is with a voice that comes from all throats at once.

When they speak, it is with one voice that comes from all throats at once.

### 11 The Enlightened › Bases and Means

- alt: Quiet street of brick terraced houses. The house is inconspicuous, the front garden overgrown. In the cellar lies a circle of chalk and ash, about two metres in diameter, with a fist-sized crystal in the middle. The circle is warm. In the living room lie books of the Enlightened, candles, notes. In the garden, remains of ash. Maarten de Vries stays there, sometimes with one or two followers from the forum whom he has initiated. The door is usually locked. A window on the ground floor is loose. The cellar has a small window to the garden that can be opened from outside with a Security Systems test against Doable (2).
- neu: Quiet street of brick terraced houses. The house is inconspicuous, the front garden overgrown. In the living room lie books of the Enlightened, candles, notes. In the garden, remains of ash. Maarten de Vries stays there, sometimes with one or two followers from the forum whom he has initiated. The door is usually locked. A ground-floor window is loose. The cellar has a small window to the garden that can be opened from outside with a Security Systems test against Doable (2).

- alt: A small café opposite the terraced house, with a view of the street. The Brussels group has its regulars’ table there. On the wall hangs a framed print with an eye in a triangle. The landlord knows nothing, but he knows the faces. Nadia El-Khoury is there, sometimes Thomas Peeters, occasionally other members. Whoever poses as a member gets information at the regulars’ table, with a Deception test against Doable (2).

Anyone who passes themselves off as a member gets information at the regulars’ table with a Deception test against Doable (2).

- alt: In the station’s locker area, level -1, near the exit to Avenue Fonsny. It holds a copy of the ritual “The Second Light”, a small crystal from Maarten’s cellar and a notebook belonging to Thomas Peeters. Thomas Peeters has the key. Without the key, opening the lock takes a Security Systems test against Hard (3).

There lie a copy of the ritual “The Second Light”, a small crystal from Maarten’s cellar and a notebook belonging to Thomas Peeters. Thomas Peeters has the key. Without the key, opening the lock requires a Security Systems test against Hard (3).

- alt: An old town house in Vieux Lyon, with a heavy wooden door and an inner courtyard. Inside, a meeting room with candles, seals and a large table. In a cupboard lie ritual books, among them a copy of “The Second Light”. In the cellar a painted circle that is cold. Hieronymus Falkenrath and his innermost circle, about six people, stay there. The door is locked. An agent can pose as a member, with a Deception test against Hard (3). The back entrance through the courtyard is easier, Security Systems against Doable (2).

The door is locked. An agent can pass themselves off as a member with a Deception test against Hard (3). The back entrance via the inner courtyard is easier, Security Systems against Doable (2).

- alt: Money: Little. The group funds itself through membership fees, about 200 euros a month. Maarten de Vries lives on his savings. Falkenrath has his own fortune and finances the circle in Lyon. ¶ People: In Brussels about twelve active members, five of whom Maarten considers initiated. Across Europe the Print Shop estimates a thousand people who feel they belong to the Enlightened. Falkenrath has about twenty followers in Lyon. ¶ Equipment: Books of the Print Shop, candles, chalk, ash, a crystal from a rift find in the Ardennes. No weapons except knives and a pistol carried by Falkenrath. ¶ Artefacts: The Signet Ring with the Eye (Game Master’s Book, chapter Artefacts) is the original from which the Print Shop had a hundred copies made in 1968. Maarten wears a copy. Falkenrath wears a copy and claims it is the original. ¶ Connections: The forum “Illuminati Europa” with about 200 registered members. A dead drop in an antiquarian bookshop in the Rue Blaes, set up by the Print Shop and controlled by Georg Wendlinger. Falkenrath has contact with a collector in Lyon who procures crystals for him.

### 11 The Enlightened › The Plan: “The Second Light”

- alt: Maarten de Vries wants to complete the ritual. He believes the “Second Light” is an enlightenment. In truth, the circle opens a threshold through which something wants to return. The cell notices it first through the missing-person reports, then through the warmth in the cellar and the humming that Psions nearby hear.
- neu: Maarten de Vries wants to complete the ritual. He believes the “Second Light” is an enlightenment. In truth, the circle opens a threshold through which something wants to return. The cell first notices it from the missing persons reports, then from the warmth in the cellar and the hum that Psions nearby hear.

- alt: ○○○○○ Maarten de Vries reads the ritual and decides to perform it. Trail: A forum post in which he asks about “real crystals”. ¶ ☠○○○○ Maarten buys a crystal on the internet and paints the first circle in his cellar. Trail: A postman notices the smell of ash in the stairwell. ¶ ☠☠○○○ Maarten invites Sophie Renard, Jonas Vermeulen and Aylin Demir. The ritual takes place. Three people disappear. Trail: The Belgian police take missing-person reports. An O.D.I.N. observer intercepts the reports. Grey File “The Second Light”.
- neu: ○○○○○ Maarten de Vries reads the ritual and decides to perform it. Sign: A forum post in which he asks for “real crystals”. ¶ ☠○○○○ Maarten buys a crystal online and paints the first circle in his cellar. Trail: A postman notices the smell of ash in the stairwell. ¶ ☠☠○○○ Maarten invites Sophie Renard, Jonas Vermeulen and Aylin Demir. The ritual takes place. Three people disappear. Sign: The Belgian police take missing persons reports. An O.D.I.N. observer intercepts the reports. Grey File “The Second Light”.

- alt: ☠☠☠☠○ The ritual begins to work on its own. The circle pulses, the crystal hums. Maarten hears the Voice. Trail: A Psion in Brussels hears humming from the direction of Anderlecht. A Scientist with a field scanner measures a beat that resembles the knocking of the Opener. Red File. ¶ ☠☠☠☠☠ The Second Light happens. The circle opens. Something steps through. Maarten becomes a gate. Trail: The street in Anderlecht lies in silence through the night. All clocks within 100 metres stop. Black File. ¶ The Key Figure is Maarten de Vries. If the cell takes him out before the fifth skull is reached, the plan loses two skulls. After the next mission the game master does not roll for this plan.
- neu: ☠☠☠☠○ The ritual begins to work on its own. The circle pulses, the crystal hums. Maarten hears the Voice. Sign: A Psion in Brussels hears a hum from the direction of Anderlecht. A Scientist with a field scanner measures a beat that resembles the knocking of the Opener. Red File. ¶ ☠☠☠☠☠ The Second Light happens. The circle opens. Something steps through. Maarten becomes a gate. Sign: The street in Anderlecht lies in silence that night. All clocks within 100 metres stop. Black File. ¶ The Key Figure is Maarten de Vries. If the cell eliminates him before the fifth skull is reached, the plan loses two skulls. After the next mission the game master does not roll for this plan.

- alt: Georg Wendlinger notices that O.D.I.N. is investigating in Brussels. He erases the Print Shop’s traces and warns Maarten via a dead drop. Maarten de Vries intensifies the ritual, paints the circle larger and brings two followers into the house to guard him. Nadia El-Khoury sends a message to all members: “They are among us. Delete your profiles.” The group scatters.
- neu: Georg Wendlinger notices that O.D.I.N. is investigating in Brussels. He erases the traces of the Print Shop and warns Maarten via a dead letter box. Maarten de Vries strengthens the ritual, paints the circle larger and brings two followers into the house to guard him. Nadia El-Khoury sends a message to all members: “They are among us. Delete your profiles.” The group disperses.

- alt: At ☠☠☠☠○ : “The Page from 1871” (Lodge of the Silent Circle) gains a skull. The Lodge recognises the song of 1871 in the ritual in Anderlecht and believes the time has come. ¶ At ☠☠☠☠☠ : “The Staircase” (the Singers) gains a skull. The Singers hear the song from Anderlecht and find another staircase.

### 11 The Enlightened › History and Goals

- alt: 1968: The Print Shop in Zurich creates the legend “The Enlightened”: a supposed successor order of the Illuminati, with membership lists, rituals, books and later websites. Purpose: to soak up rumours about O.D.I.N. and steer them into ridicule. ¶ 1971: In an internal compendium of the Print Shop the ritual “The Second Light” appears, copied from the manuscript MS Arcana 114 of the Collegium. The Print Shop uses the seventh circle as an ornament. The Typesetter notes in the margin: “Decoration. Do not use.”
- neu: 1968: The Print Shop in Zurich creates the legend “The Enlightened”: an alleged successor order of the Illuminati, with membership lists, rituals, books and later websites. Purpose: to absorb rumours about O.D.I.N. and turn them into ridicule. ¶ 1971: In an internal compendium of the Print Shop the ritual “The Second Light” appears, copied from the manuscript MS Arcana 114 of the Collegium. The Print Shop uses the seventh circle as ornament. The Typesetter notes in the margin: “Decoration. Do not use.”

- alt: 2003: The first website of the Enlightened goes online, run by the Print Shop. In forums, independent groups form that believe the legend is real. ¶ 2011: The Accumulation. Some rituals from the books of the Enlightened suddenly work, among them circles from MS Arcana 114. The Print Shop tries to take the books out of circulation, but there are too many copies.
- neu: 2003: The first website of the Enlightened goes online, operated by the Print Shop. In forums, independent groups emerge that consider the legend real. ¶ 2011: The Accumulation. Some rituals from the books of the Enlightened suddenly work, including circles from MS Arcana 114. The Print Shop tries to remove the books from circulation, but there are too many copies.

- alt: What only the game master knows: the Print Shop never officially informed the Operations Branch of the extent. A legend writer named Georg Wendlinger has looked after the Enlightened since 2012, because he considered the legend controllable. He now knows that he was wrong.
- neu: What only the game master knows: The Print Shop never officially informed the Operations Branch about the extent. A legend writer named Georg Wendlinger has looked after the Enlightened since 2012, because he considered the legend controllable. He now knows that he was wrong.

- alt: They believe they are in the right because they take seriously the signs that others merely smile at as conspiracy. They consider themselves the few who know, who see through the Veil.
- neu: They believe they are in the right because they take seriously the signs that others only mock as conspiracy. They consider themselves the few who know, who see through the Veil.

### 11 The Enlightened › Overview

- alt: The Enlightened are a secret society invented by the Print Shop that has long since had real members and whose decorative rituals have partly worked since 2011. The Operations Branch cannot bury the legend because a thousand people carry it. For the game master they are an adversary that arose from a mistake by one’s own organisation. They show what happens when a legend takes on a life of its own.
- neu: The Enlightened are a secret society invented by the Print Shop that has long had real members and whose decorative rituals have partially worked since 2011. The Operations Branch cannot bury the legend because a thousand people carry it. For the game master they are an adversary that arose from a mistake by one’s own organisation. They show what happens when a legend takes on a life of its own.

- alt: Type Secret society, arisen from a legend of the Print Shop
- neu: Type Secret society, created from a legend of the Print Shop

### 11 The Enlightened › Key Figures

- alt: Slim, grey suit, reading glasses on a chain, looks like a man who has seen nothing but files for years. He maintains websites, forums and membership lists, deletes dangerous posts and watches the circles. He wants to stabilise the legend without the Council or the public learning how out of control it is. He conceals that in 2021 he falsified an internal report on working rituals in order to prevent the burial of the legend. He speaks dryly and officially, in file numbers and memos, and never says more than he has to.
- neu: Slim, grey suit, reading glasses on a chain, looks like a man who has seen only files for years. He maintains websites, forums and membership lists, deletes dangerous posts and observes the circles. He wants to stabilise the legend without the Council or the public learning how out of control it is. He conceals that he forged an internal report about functioning rituals in 2021 to prevent a burial of the legend. He speaks dryly and officially, in file references and memos, and never says more than he must.

- alt: Weakness He is no fighter. Proof of his 2021 forgery makes him the cell’s tool. ¶ Behaviour Watches, makes phone calls, deletes. Withdraws as soon as he suspects O.D.I.N. is nearby.
- neu: Weakness He is no fighter. Proof of his 2021 forgery makes him a tool of the cell. ¶ Behaviour Watches, makes phone calls, deletes. Withdraws as soon as he suspects O.D.I.N.

- alt: Pale, unshaven, dark rings under his eyes, wears a shirt that is too big and a ring with an eye. He has performed the ritual “The Second Light” and believes the three who vanished have been “enlightened”. He wants to complete the ritual and see the “Second Light”. He conceals that he invited the three guests and that he repaints the circle every night. He speaks quietly, with long pauses, as if he were listening to someone.
- neu: Pale, unshaven, dark circles under the eyes, wears an oversized shirt and a ring with an eye. He has performed the ritual “The Second Light” and believes the three missing people were “enlightened”. He wants to complete the ritual and see the “Second Light”. He conceals that he invited the three guests and that he repaints the circle every night. He speaks quietly, with long pauses, as if listening to someone.

- alt: Special If he stands in the circle, he rolls +1 die on all tests. The circle protects him until it is sealed. ¶ Weakness Proof that the ritual is lying to him. A Negotiator who speaks his language. He is no fighter.
- neu: Special When he stands in the circle, he rolls +1 die on all tests. The circle protects him until it is sealed. ¶ Weakness Proof that the ritual lies to him. A Negotiator who speaks his language. He is no fighter.

- alt: Short dark hair, leather jacket, always has her computer with her, looks like an overworked project manager. She founded the group and manages the membership lists. She wants to protect the group, thinks the ritual is a role-playing game and wants no scandal. She conceals that she herself once drew a small circle from a book of the Enlightened, but never performed a ritual, and that she fears something might have noticed it anyway. She speaks fast, with lots of English words, and interrupts others.
- neu: Short dark hair, leather jacket, always carries her computer, looks like an overworked project manager. She founded the group and manages the membership lists. She wants to protect the group, considers the ritual a role-playing game and wants no scandal. She conceals that she once drew a small circle from a book of the Enlightened herself, but never performed a ritual, and that she fears something may have noticed it anyway. - alt: Burly, short hair, wears an old German army jacket, looks like a man who has been hiding for a long time. He was a member of the Brussels group until October 2026 and withdrew after the three vanished. He wants to forget what he saw, and wants no witness statement, no contact, no file. He conceals that he stole a copy of the ritual and a small crystal from Maarten’s cellar, both deposited in a locker at Brussels-South station. He speaks in clipped phrases, suspiciously, and repeats himself.
- neu: Stocky, short hair, wears an old Bundeswehr jacket, looks like a man who has hidden for a long time. He was a member of the Brussels group until October 2026 and withdrew after the disappearance of the three. He wants to forget what he saw, and wants no witness statement, no contact, no file. He conceals that he stole a copy of the ritual and a small crystal from Maarten’s cellar, both deposited in a locker at Brussels-South station. He speaks in clipped phrases, suspiciously, and repeats himself.

- alt: Tall, silver hair, expensive coat, wears a signet ring with an eye on his right hand. He considers the order real and believes the Print Shop is merely a legend that disguises the order. He wants to lead the order out of hiding and achieve “enlightenment” for all members, and he wants the ritual from Brussels for his own circle. He conceals that he has never performed a ritual because he fears it could consume him, and that he sends others ahead. He speaks unctuously, in long sentences, and calls himself “Grand Master”.

### 11 The Enlightened › Relationship to O.D.I.N.

- alt: Who at O.D.I.N. deals with them: The Operations Branch, more precisely the Print Shop in Zurich. Georg Wendlinger is the legend writer in charge. The cell’s handler received the file “The Second Light” from an observer in Brussels who intercepted the missing-person reports.

### 11 The Enlightened › Hooks and Outlook

- alt: White File “The Ring”. An antiques dealer in Brussels reports that a man offered him a signet ring with an eye that seems “too real”. The cell is to examine the ring. It is a copy from the Print Shop, but the dealer has the seller’s name: Maarten de Vries. ¶ Grey File “The Second Light”. In Brussels, three members of the internet group “Illuminati Europa” have disappeared: Sophie Renard, 28, Jonas Vermeulen, 41, and Aylin Demir, 31. On their computers is a ritual of the Print Shop from 1971. The trail leads to the cellar of a terraced house in Anderlecht, where a circle is painted on the floor. The circle is warm. ¶ Red File “The Warm Circle”. A Psion in Brussels hears a humming from Anderlecht that is growing louder. A Scientist with a field scanner measures a beat that resembles the knocking of the Opener. The cell is to find the house and seal the circle before the ritual completes itself.

On their computers lies a ritual from the Print Shop from 1971. The trail leads to the cellar of a terraced house in Anderlecht, where a circle is painted on the floor. The circle is warm. ¶ Red File “The Warm Circle”. A Psion in Brussels hears a hum from Anderlecht that grows louder. A Scientist with a field scanner measures a beat that resembles the knocking of the Opener. The cell is to find the house and seal the circle before the ritual completes itself.

- alt: The Second Light has happened. The circle in Anderlecht has opened, and something has stepped through. Maarten de Vries is no longer a human being, but a gate. The street has been evacuated, the Belgian police speak of a gas leak. O.D.I.N. has sealed the cellar, but the humming has not fallen silent. It wanders. ¶ The next plan: “The Hundred Rings” ○○○○○ . The copies of the signet ring with the eye that the Print Shop had made in 1968 begin to work. Whoever wears a ring hears the Voice. The Enlightened are looking for the rings.

- alt: If the cell has played Grey File 8 “The Second Light”, the following applies depending on the outcome: if the circle was sealed, the plan drops to ☠☠○○○ . If Maarten was also arrested, the Key Figure is taken out: the plan drops to ☠○○○○ and does not roll after the next mission. If Maarten escaped or the ritual was not ended, the plan stays at ☠☠☠○○ and Maarten keeps painting the circle. If the crystal was handed over to O.D.I.N., the plan loses another skull, because the ritual cannot be completed without the crystal. If the forum data was handed over to the Print Shop, the Print Shop uses it for a counter-legend and the plan loses a skull.

If the forum data was handed over to the Print Shop, the Print Shop uses it for a counter-legend and the plan loses a skull.

### 09 The Freemasons and the Legend of the Illuminati › The Freemasons

- alt: The vast majority of Freemasons are what they appear to be: people who meet regularly, maintain old rituals, do charity work and talk about ethics. They have nothing to do with O.D.I.N. They know nothing of the Veil, of the rifts, of the children. Their Knowledge is 1: they know their own rituals, their archives, their members. Nothing more. ¶ For O.D.I.N. the Freemasons are no threat and no adversary. Lodges are closed spaces with secrecy, symbols and old archives. That is exactly what makes them an ideal hiding place for someone who wants to conceal something, and a good cover for agents. Some handlers are members of a lodge because one meets people there whom one would not otherwise meet: judges, entrepreneurs, doctors, police officers. The federation itself knows nothing of this. ¶ The Freemasons have no plans. They appear in the table “Status 2026” without skulls, because they intend nothing that concerns O.D.I.N. Their Strength is 4, because the federation is large, old and well connected. But this Strength is not a weapon. It is a tool that others can use.

The federation itself knows nothing of this. ¶ The Freemasons have no plans. They appear in the “Status 2026” table without skulls because they intend nothing that concerns O.D.I.N. Their strength is 4 because the fraternity is large, old and well connected. But this Strength is not a weapon. It is a tool that others can use.

### 09 The Freemasons and the Legend of the Illuminati › The Legend of the Illuminati

- alt: What came after is a legend. Since the late 18th century, the Illuminati have been held responsible for every conspiracy one can imagine: the French Revolution, world wars, financial crises, pop music. For O.D.I.N., that is a gift. Anyone who researches strange incidents eventually ends up with the Illuminati. And anyone who ends up with the Illuminati is no longer taken seriously. ¶ The Illuminati have Strength 0 and Knowledge 0, because they do not exist. They are a cover story that serves O.D.I.N. The Print Shop in Zurich has cultivated the legend since 1968, writes books, websites, membership lists. All invented. All there to swallow real trails.
- neu: What came after is a legend. Since the late 18th century the Illuminati have been blamed for every conspiracy one can imagine: the French Revolution, world wars, financial crises, pop music. For O.D.I.N., that is a gift. Anyone who researches strange incidents eventually ends up with the Illuminati. And anyone who ends up with the Illuminati is no longer taken seriously. ¶ The Illuminati have Strength 0 and Knowledge 0, because they do not exist. They are a cover story that benefits O.D.I.N. The Print Shop in Zurich has cultivated the legend since 1968, writing books, websites, membership lists. All invented. All there to swallow real trails.

### 09 The Freemasons and the Legend of the Illuminati › Overview

- alt: The Freemasons are a worldwide federation of lodges, founded in the early 18th century, with rituals, degrees, symbols and a long tradition of secrecy. For O.D.I.N. they are no threat and no adversary, but a backdrop, a place where something can hide. The Illuminati, on the other hand, are a legend that O.D.I.N. has cultivated for decades to swallow real trails. Both turn up in missions when the cell follows a false trail or needs a cover.
- neu: The Freemasons are a worldwide fraternity of lodges, founded in the early 18th century, with rituals, degrees, symbols and a long tradition of secrecy. For O.D.I.N. they are not a threat and not an opponent, but a backdrop, a place where something can hide. The Illuminati, on the other hand, are a legend that O.D.I.N. has cultivated for decades to swallow real trails. Both appear in missions when the cell follows a false trail or needs a cover.

### 09 The Freemasons and the Legend of the Illuminati › Hooks

- alt: White File “Set Square”. A retired dentist in Hanover has reported to the police that someone broke into his lodge and stole nothing except a book of minutes from 1872. The police consider the case trivial. An observer of O.D.I.N. intercepted the report because the year 1872 appears in an open file. The cell is to find out whether it matters. It is a day’s work. The lodge in Hanover is a daughter of the lodge in Uppsala. The stolen book contains a copy of the founding charter of the Lodge of the Silent Circle. Whoever stole it is a messenger from Uppsala, a former archivist who works for the lodge and does not know what he is carrying. He was sent to fetch it and knows only the assignment, not the content. The cell finds him in a hotel in Hanover, with the book in his suitcase. ¶ White File “The Illuminatus”. In an internet forum a user called “Weishaupt1776” claims to be a direct descendant of the founder and to own the real signet ring with the eye. He offers to sell memberships, for 500 euros each. The Print Shop has discovered the post and wants to know whether the man is just a fraudster or whether he owns something real. The cell is to visit him in Leipzig, where he lives in a small flat, surrounded by printed-out websites. The ring is one of the hundred copies the Print Shop had made in 1968, bought at a flea market. The man is harmless. But in his flat lies a book he should not have: a print by the Print Shop from 1971, with a real circle as an ornament. ¶ White File “The Lodge Brother”. A member of the Lodge of the Closed Eye in Hanover, an estate agent, told another member that in an old house in Hildesheim he found a basement in which a circle is painted on the floor. The circle is warm, he said. The other member is Cornelius Weber. He reports the find to the Print Shop, which opens a White File. The cell is to check the basement. The circle is painted in red paint, by teenagers who held a party there. The warmth comes from a defective heating pipe. The case is a red herring. But in the house next door lives an old woman who for a week has been singing a song at night that nobody knows.

The police consider the case insignificant. An Observer from O.D.I.N. intercepted the report because the year 1872 appears in an open file. The cell is to find out whether it matters. It is a day’s work. The lodge in Hanover is a daughter of the lodge in Uppsala. The stolen book contains a copy of the founding charter of the Lodge of the Silent Circle. The person who stole it is a messenger from Uppsala, a former archivist who works for the lodge and does not know what he carries. He was sent to fetch it and knows only the assignment, not the content. The cell finds him in a hotel in Hanover, with the book in his suitcase. ¶ White File “The Illuminatus”. In an internet forum, a user named “Weishaupt1776” claims to be a direct descendant of the founder and to own the genuine signet ring with the eye. He offers to sell memberships for €500 each. The Print Shop has discovered the post and wants to know whether the man is merely a fraud or whether he owns something genuine. The cell is to visit him in Leipzig, where he lives in a small flat, surrounded by printed-out websites. But in his flat lies a book that he should not have: a print from the Print Shop from 1971, with a real circle as an ornament. ¶ White File “The Lodge Brother”. A member of the Lodge of the Sealed Eye in Hanover, an estate agent, has told another member that he found a cellar in an old house in Hildesheim in which a circle is painted on the floor. The circle is warm, he said. The other member is Cornelius Weber. He reports the find to the Print Shop, which opens a White File. The cell is to check the basement. The circle is painted with red paint, by young people who held a party there. The warmth comes from a defective heating pipe. The case is a red herring. But in the neighbouring house lives an old woman who for a week has been singing at night a song that no one knows.

### 09 The Freemasons and the Legend of the Illuminati › A Handler in the Lodge

- alt: Weber has been a Freemason since 2008, admitted on the instructions of the Print Shop, which built a legend for him: a retired judge who, after the death of his wife, is looking for a circle that gives him support. The legend is so good that in the lodge Weber is regarded as a quiet, reliable brother who rarely speaks and listens well. ¶ What Weber learns in the lodge is rarely spectacular. He hears about property sales, about inheritance disputes, about a member who set up a foundation in Basel. He hears about a brother who worked for a British service in the nineties and now lives in Hamburg. He hears about an old minute book that has stood in the lodge library since 1872 and that nobody may read, because it comes from another lodge. The rumour says it contains the founding charter of a lodge that no longer exists. Weber has never seen the book. ¶ Once a year Weber reports to the Print Shop. Most of the information is worthless. But in October 2026 he heard about a member who knows an accountant in Brussels who has suddenly disappeared. The accountant was a member of an internet group called “Illuminati Europa”. Weber reported the name. The report only confirmed for the Print Shop a trail it already had. The Grey File “The Second Light” itself came about through an observer in Brussels who intercepted the missing-person reports of the Belgian police, because on the missing people’s computers there was a ritual written by the Print Shop in 1971.

### 09 The Freemasons and the Legend of the Illuminati › In Play: The Red Herring

- alt: Freemasons are almost always a red herring. A cell finds a set square at a crime scene and believes it has found a conspiracy. It has found a neighbour who goes to his lodge on Tuesday evenings. That is exactly what makes the one exception so dangerous: the Lodge of the Silent Circle, which is covered in the next dossier. All other lodges are what they appear to be. ¶ The following table provides six finds that look like a conspiracy and are not. They are suitable for White Files, for interludes or for false trails in larger missions.
- neu: Freemasons are almost always a red herring. A cell finds a set square at a crime scene and believes it has found a conspiracy. It has found a neighbour who goes to his lodge on Tuesday evenings. That is exactly what makes the one exception so dangerous: the Lodge of the Silent Circle, which is covered in the next dossier. All other lodges are what they seem. ¶ The following table provides six finds that look like a conspiracy and are not. They are suitable for White Files, for interlude scenes, or for false trails in larger missions.

- alt: 1 A pair of compasses and a set square made of brass, found in the flat of a missing accountant. The man was a Freemason, for twenty years. The find is an heirloom from his grandfather, not a clue. ¶ 2 A calendar entry “Lodge evening, 19:30” on a witness’s mobile phone. The witness is a member of a regular lodge in Hanover. The evening was a lecture on local history.

The evening was a lecture on local history.

- alt: 5 An empty room in a basement with a black-and-white tiled floor, in the middle a table, on it an open book. The room is a lodge’s practice room. The book is a set of minutes from 1998. ¶ 6 A man who enters a lodge house at night with a suitcase and leaves again without it. The suitcase contains a donation for a children’s hospice. The man is the treasurer.

The man is the treasurer.

### 06 Department 13 › Relationship to O.D.I.N.

- alt: Who at O.D.I.N. deals with them: the handler of Hold-5 in Vienna, who watches the Department’s movements in Eastern Europe. The Echo operatives, who have had a score to settle with the Department since 1976. And the Audit, which checks every hint of a mole in the Swedish mining authority. ¶ O.D.I.N. knows that Department 13 has existed since the fifties, that it has access to Siberian restricted zones, that it uses up psions without helmets and that it is searching for a second site for Alloy S. O.D.I.N. does not know that the Department has been drilling at Kola-7 since 2019.
- neu: Those at O.D.I.N. who deal with them: the handler of Hold-5 in Vienna, who observes the Department’s movements in Eastern Europe. The Echo-Operatives, who have had an open score with the Department since 1976. And the Audit, which checks every indication of a mole in the Swedish mining authority. ¶ O.D.I.N. knows that Department 13 has existed since the fifties, that it has access to Siberian restricted zones, that it uses up Psions without helmets and that it is searching for a second site of Alloy S. O.D.I.N. does not know that the Department has been drilling in Kola-7 since 2019.

- alt: The Department offers an agent to bring him and his family to Russia, with real papers and a new life. In return it wants the coordinates of an O.D.I.N. archive in which Alloy S is stored, or a psion who defects.
- neu: The Department offers to bring an agent and their family to Russia, with real papers and a new life. In return, it wants the coordinates of an O.D.I.N. archive where Alloy S is stored, or a Psion who defects.

### 06 Department 13 › Bases and Means

- alt: Moscow, Ulitsa Gospitalnaya 7. A five-storey building from the fifties, officially an archive of an invented cover agency for healthcare. The basement has been built out, with a silence, an interrogation room and a room in which the Tunguska fragments are stored. This is where the Department’s administration sits, along with the files of the search for the second site and the fragments, about forty pieces, the largest as big as a forearm. Orlowa works there when she is not at Kola-7, along with twelve to fifteen administrative staff who officially work as archivists. You get in through the back entrance with a service pass from the cover agency, or through the basement, which is connected to Baumanskaya metro station by an old maintenance tunnel. ¶ Restricted zone “Kola-7”. Kola Peninsula, about 80 kilometres southeast of Murmansk, in a disused mine from the Tsarist era. Officially the area is closed because of the danger of collapse. This is where the Department suspects the vein of alloy ore that the first measurements pointed to in 2019, and there is also a camp of residential containers, a workshop for the golems and a shaft that leads down into the depths where the vein lies. On site are about thirty workers and technicians, ten hunters, two psions without helmets and a Golem-7 that guards the entrance (stats in the Threat Atlas). You get in by an unpaved road that begins near Murmansk and passes two checkpoints, or through the old shaft, which can be reached from the lake if you can dive. ¶ Restricted zone “Ural-3”. In the northern Urals, in a valley that has officially been closed since 1963 because of “geological instability”. The Department has a base there for the search for Alloy S. An abandoned village from the fifties serves as the base, along with a tunnel system in which the Department searches for ore, and a room in which the psions without helmets sleep. On site are ten hunters, four psions without helmets and a geologist team under Orlowa when she is there. You get in by a pass road that is impassable in winter, or by the river that flows through the valley, if you have a boat and survive the rapids.

- alt: Money. The Department finances itself by selling crystal shards from Siberian restricted zones to collectors, among them the Evening. Estimated annual budget: 40 to 60 million euros, a third of it for the search for Alloy S. ¶ People. About 100 people, of them 40 hunters, 30 technicians and geologists, 20 administrative staff and 8 psions without helmets. In addition there are local hands who work as drivers, cooks and guards without knowing for whom. ¶ Equipment. Three golems of the Golem-7 type, two of them operational (stats in the Threat Atlas). A dozen off-road vehicles, two Mi-8 helicopters that officially belong to a salvage company. Ammunition with Siberian crystal dust, which acts like incendiary ammunition against beings from rifts. ¶ Artefacts. The Tunguska fragments, about forty pieces, the largest as big as a forearm. They are warm, hum softly and power any electrical device to which they are connected. O.D.I.N. keeps a single piece in Bern, and the Department wants it back. ¶ Connections. A mole in the Swedish mining authority who supplies information about Kiruna. A contact at the Evening who buys crystal shards and supplies information about O.D.I.N. in return. A former officer of Grey Meridian who works for the Department when the price is right.

### 06 Department 13 › The Plan: “The Second Site”

- alt: Department 13 has been searching for a second site for Alloy S for decades. In 2019 it found it. In the restricted zone “Kola-7” the Department is mining a vein of ore that is silent. The cell first notices it in small things: a contact in Murmansk goes silent, an order for beryllium runs through a shell company, and in Vienna a hunter turns up with a helmet that is not an O.D.I.N. model. Then the radio traffic is intercepted.
- neu: Department 13 has been searching for a second site of Alloy S for decades. In 2019 it found it. In the restricted zone “Kola-7” the Department is mining a vein of ore that is silent. The cell first notices it through small things: a contact in Murmansk goes silent, an order for beryllium runs through a shell company, and in Vienna a Hunter appears with a helmet that is not an O.D.I.N. Then the radio traffic is intercepted.

- alt: ○○○○○ The Department searches between Kola and the Urals for Alloy S. The search is systematic, but unsuccessful. Trail: None. The Department works in secret. ¶ ☠○○○○ A geologist team finds a vein of ore in Kola-7 that is silent. The Department begins mining. Trail: A dead drop in Murmansk contains a message in Russian: “The silence has a price.” The handler of Hold-5 considers it bait. ¶ ☠☠○○○ The Department expands the mining and begins to smelt the ore. The first ingots of Alloy S are produced. Trail: A newspaper article about an “unusual find” in the Murmansk region disappears from the archive after two days. A contact in the canteen of Hold-5 says the Russians have “found something that is silent”. ¶ ☠☠☠○○ The Department has enough ore to build a first helmet. It is crude, but it works. Orlowa wears it at a demonstration in Moscow. Trail: A radio message from the Department is intercepted: “The lid is on.” The Audit comes across an order of beryllium that went to a shell company in Murmansk. ¶ ☠☠☠☠○ The Department begins to train its own psions with the new helmets. The first three survive longer than two years. Trail: A hunter of the Department is seen in Vienna, with a helmet that is not an O.D.I.N. model. A psion of the cell senses a silence that moves. ¶ ☠☠☠☠☠ Department 13 has alloy ore of its own in sufficient quantity. It no longer needs helmets from Europe, and O.D.I.N. no longer has a monopoly on the lid. Trail: A radio message to O.D.I.N., open and unencrypted: “We have the silence. You have the fear.” The Tunguska shard in Bern begins to hum louder. ¶ The Key Figure of the plan is Dr. Larissa Orlowa. She leads the geological analysis, knows the coordinates of Kola-7 and drives the mining forward, because she wants to find the site her father never found. If the cell takes Orlowa out, the plan loses two skulls. The Department does not roll after the next mission, because without Orlowa the search stalls and the hunters under Tarasow cannot continue the mining at the same pace.

- alt: If the cell takes a skull from the plan, Department 13 answers with a countermove.
- neu: If the cell takes a skull from the plan, Department 13 responds with a countermove.

- alt: 1 to 2 A contact of the cell suddenly goes silent. He was visited by a hunter of the Department and was given a message: “The silence is listening.” The contact is afraid and no longer wants to talk to O.D.I.N. ¶ 3 to 4 A piece of bait turns up: a dead drop with a message that supposedly comes from a defector from the Department. The message contains a false coordinate in the restricted zone “Ural-3”. Whoever follows it walks into an ambush.

- alt: At ☠☠☠☠○ : “A Seeker of Their Own” (STILLWATER) gains a skull. The Americans fear coming away empty-handed on alloy ore, and speed up their own programme. ¶ At ☠☠☠☠☠ : “The Great Stone” (The Evening) loses a skull. The Evening loses the Department as supplier and bidder.

### 06 Department 13 › Key Figures

- alt: A gaunt woman of 52 with grey hair that she ties back severely, and steel-rimmed glasses that she constantly takes off and puts back on. She leads the search for the second site and is the driving force behind the plan. She is the daughter of the officer who was meant to recruit the Open Ones in 1976 and came back empty-handed, and she wants to find the site her father never found. She conceals that in 1981 her father left her a notebook containing the name of the fifteen-year-old whom the Echo operatives arrested. She speaks briefly, precisely, without polite phrases. She says “we” and means the Department, never herself.
- neu: A gaunt woman of 52 with grey hair tied back severely and steel-framed glasses that she constantly takes off and puts back on. She leads the search for the second site and is the driving force behind the plan. She is the daughter of the officer who was supposed to recruit the Open Ones in 1976 and came back empty-handed, and she wants to find the site her father never found. She conceals that her father left her a notebook in 1981 containing the name of the fifteen-year-old whom the Echo-Operatives arrested. She speaks briefly, precisely, without pleasantries. She says “we” and means the Department, never herself.

- alt: Special Knows the restricted zones between Kola and the Urals better than any other person. In negotiations, she can call in a Golem-7 with a radio message (stats in the Threat Atlas). ¶ Weakness Her father’s notebook. Whoever mentions it throws her off her stride. Whoever has it has her. ¶ Behaviour She does not negotiate, she sets conditions. She withdraws when the plan is endangered, and for that she sacrifices even her own people.
- neu: Special Knows the restricted zones between Kola and the Urals better than any other person. Can request a Golem-7 in negotiations with a radio message (stats in the Threat Atlas). ¶ Weakness Her father’s notebook. Whoever mentions it throws her off. Whoever has it has her. ¶ Behaviour She does not negotiate, she sets conditions. She withdraws when the plan is endangered, even sacrificing her own people for it.

- alt: A heavy-set man of 70 with close-cropped grey hair, an old scar over his left eye and hands that look as if they could bend iron. He leads the hunters of Department 13, the task forces for restricted zones and for operations abroad. In 1976 he was in the Carpathians as a young soldier and saw the operation fail. He wants to erase the disgrace of 1976, which he blames on the Department, and he wants to prevent Orlowa from leading the Department into a war it cannot win. He conceals that in 1976 he was glad when the Europeans were faster, because he did not want to carry out the order he had on him. He speaks in a military manner, curtly, with a deep voice that rarely rises. He says “order” and “carried out”, even when he objects.
- neu: A heavy-set man of 70 with close-cropped grey hair, an old scar over his left eye and hands that look as if they could bend iron. He leads the hunters of Department 13, the task forces for restricted zones and for operations abroad. He was there in the Carpathians in 1976 as a young soldier and saw how the operation failed. He wants to erase the disgrace of 1976, which he blames on the Department, and he wants to prevent Orlowa from leading the Department into a war it cannot win. He conceals that in 1976 he was glad when the Europeans were faster, because he did not want to carry out the order he was carrying. He speaks militarily, tersely, with a deep voice that rarely gets louder. He says “order” and “carried out”, even when he disagrees.

- alt: Special Once per fight he can order a hunter near him to make an immediate attack. ¶ Weakness His conscience. Whoever proves to him that the Department uses up its young psions gains an ally, or at least a waverer. ¶ Behaviour He leads from the front, but he is no fanatic. He withdraws when the mission can no longer be justified, and he protects his people, even against Orlowa.
- neu: Special Once per combat, he can order a Hunter near him to make an immediate attack. ¶ Weakness His conscience. Whoever proves to him that the Department uses up its young Psions gains an ally, or at least a hesitant one. ¶ Behaviour He leads from the front, but he is no fanatic. He withdraws when the mission is no longer justifiable, and he protects his people, even against Orlowa.

- alt: A young woman of 22 with a shaved head, pale skin and a constant slight nosebleed. She wears a grey smock, and no shoes when she is not on an operation. She is the Department’s strongest psion, a telekinetic who was found two years ago in the restricted zone “Ural-3”, when she was twenty. She works for the Department because she was told that otherwise O.D.I.N. would come for her. She wants to survive, and to know whether the voice she hears really is the Thirteenth or something else. She conceals that she once put on an O.D.I.N. helmet and the voice was gone for an hour. She speaks softly, with long pauses, as if she first had to fetch every word out of the silence. She says “we” and means the psions, never the Department.

She says “we” and means the psions, never the Department.

- alt: Stats Psion without helmet (Game Master’s Book, Threat Atlas, subtype of the Hunter of Department 13), a telekinetic ¶ Weakness Alloy S blocks her completely. A psion with a helmet senses her like a patch of light in a dark room. ¶ Behaviour She fights when ordered to, but she does not seek a fight. She watches, listens and disappears when the Department does not need her.

She watches, listens and disappears when the Department does not need her.

### 06 Department 13 › Overview

- alt: Department 13 is what O.D.I.N. would have become if there had never been a Hallström, never a Marek and never a Council. A programme in Russian services that uses up psions, exploits restricted zones and has been searching for over forty years for the one site that would take the monopoly on Alloy S away from O.D.I.N. For the game master, the Department is the enemy with state cover, which works where O.D.I.N. cannot reach and knows exactly what a psion without a helmet is worth.
- neu: Department 13 is what O.D.I.N. would have become if there had never been a Hallström, never a Marek, never a Council. A programme in Russian service that uses up Psions, exploits restricted zones and has been searching for over forty years for the one find site that would take O.D.I.N.’s monopoly on Alloy S. For the game master, the Department is the enemy with state cover, working where O.D.I.N. cannot reach, and knowing exactly what a Psion without a helmet is worth.

- alt: Seat Moscow, administrative seat in an inconspicuous building on Ulitsa Gospitalnaya; operational base in the restricted zone “Kola-7” on the Kola Peninsula
- neu: Seat Moscow, administrative headquarters in an inconspicuous building on Uliza Gospitalnaja; operational base in the restricted zone “Kola-7” on the Kola Peninsula

### 06 Department 13 › History and Goals

- alt: 1954. In the Soviet Union, a working group for “psychotronics” is set up under the umbrella of an invented cover agency for heavy industry. Officially it is about influencing people through electromagnetic fields. In fact the group collects reports about children who know things they cannot know. The name “Department 13” first appears in an internal memo. ¶ 1957. From an archive of the Soviet security apparatus, Department 13 receives finds from the Tunguska expedition of 1927: dark metal fragments, warm, softly humming, without any loss of energy. The fragments come from the site of the explosion of 1908. The Department begins to understand that there are materials that do not fit into the known order. What exactly the fragments are remains open. ¶ 1963. Department 13 begins the systematic exploration of restricted zones on Kola and in the Urals. The first expeditions end in losses. Within two years the Department loses fourteen wearers of heavy protective suits. The programme is continued nevertheless, because the restricted zones cannot be entered without heavy equipment. ¶ 1976. In the Carpathians, Department 13 intends to win over seven Open Ones. The operation fails when Echo operatives under Paul Mercier arrest the Open Ones without firing a shot. One of those arrested is fifteen years old. Since that day every O.D.I.N. agent on Russian territory is considered an enemy combatant. What only the game master knows: Orlowa’s father was the Department officer who was meant to recruit the Open Ones in 1976 and came back empty-handed. He was killed by a Swarm in 1981 during an operation in the restricted zone “Ural-3”. ¶ 1981. The mine at Kiruna is exhausted. Department 13 learns of it through a mole in the Swedish mining authority. The conviction that there must be a second site for Alloy S becomes doctrine. Between Kola and the Urals a systematic search begins that continues to this day. ¶ 1991. After the collapse of the Soviet Union, the Russian security apparatus takes over the Department. Funding briefly collapses, then the Department finds a new source of money: selling crystal shards from Siberian restricted zones to collectors, among them the Evening. The Department keeps the best pieces. ¶ 1993. Department 13 begins building “golems”: armoured exoskeletons for operations in Siberian restricted zones. The first prototypes fail. The programme is continued nevertheless, because the restricted zones cannot be entered without heavy equipment. Most golems are later scrapped, some still run.
- neu: 1954. In the Soviet Union, a working group for “psychotronics” is created under the umbrella of an invented cover agency for heavy industry. Officially it is about influencing people through electromagnetic fields. In fact the group collects reports about children who know things they cannot know. The name “Department 13” appears for the first time in an internal memo. ¶ 1957. Department 13 receives from an archive of the Soviet security apparatus finds from the Tunguska expedition of 1927: dark metal splinters, warm, quietly humming, without energy loss. The fragments come from the site of the explosion of 1908. The Department begins to understand that there are materials that do not fit into the known order. What exactly the splinters are remains open. ¶ 1963. Department 13 begins the systematic exploration of restricted zones on Kola and in the Urals. The first expeditions end in losses. Within two years the Department loses fourteen wearers of heavy protective suits. The programme is nevertheless continued because the restricted zones cannot be entered without heavy equipment. ¶ 1976. In the Carpathians, Department 13 wants to win over seven Open Ones. The operation fails when Echo-Operatives under Paul Mercier arrest the Open Ones without a shot. One of those arrested is fifteen years old. From that day on, every O.D.I.N. agent on Russian territory is considered an enemy combatant. What only the game master knows: Orlowa’s father was an officer of the Department who was supposed to recruit the Open Ones in 1976 and came back empty-handed. He was killed in 1981 during an operation in the restricted zone “Ural-3” by a swarm. ¶ 1981. The pit near Kiruna is exhausted. Department 13 learns of this through a mole in the Swedish mining authority. The conviction that there must be a second find site for Alloy S becomes doctrine. Between Kola and the Urals a systematic search begins that continues to this day. ¶ 1991. After the collapse of the Soviet Union, the Russian security apparatus takes over the Department. Funding briefly collapses, then the Department finds a new source of money: the sale of crystal shards from Siberian restricted zones to collectors, including the Evening. The Department keeps the best pieces. ¶ 1993. Department 13 begins building “golems”: armoured exoskeletons for operations in Siberian restricted zones. The first prototypes fail. The programme is nevertheless continued because the restricted zones cannot be entered without heavy equipment. Most golems are later scrapped, some are still running.

- alt: 2019. A geologist team of Department 13 measures a spot in the rock in the restricted zone “Kola-7” that is silent. Not quiet, but silent. The Department believes it is close to the second site. The test drilling begins. What only the game master knows: the soil samples show anomalies reminiscent of Kiruna. Whether the vein lies over a thin place remains open. The Department reads the measurements as a danger, not as a certainty.
- neu: 2019. A team of geologists from Department 13 measures a spot in the rock in the restricted zone “Kola-7” that is silent. Not quiet, but silent. The Department believes it is close to the second find site. The test drilling begins. What only the game master knows: the soil samples show anomalies reminiscent of Kiruna. Whether the vein lies above a thin spot remains open. The Department reads the measurements as a danger, not as a certainty.

- alt: Department 13 wants alloy ore of its own, in order to build its own helmets and take the monopoly on the lid away from O.D.I.N. The Department’s leadership believes that O.D.I.N. gags the world’s psions with helmets in order to control them. A site of its own would allow Department 13 to train its own psions without losing them. That the Department’s psions die without helmets, the leadership considers a temporary condition, not a design flaw. ¶ The Department believes it is in the right, because it considers O.D.I.N. a European organisation that has shut Russia out of the world’s sites for decades. The internal training sessions say: “The alloy belongs to those who find it. Not to those who administer it.”
- neu: Department 13 wants alloy ore of its own, in order to build its own helmets and take the monopoly on the lid away from O.D.I.N. The Department’s leadership believes that O.D.I.N. gags the world’s psions with helmets in order to control them. A site of its own would allow Department 13 to train its own psions without losing them. The leadership considers the fact that the Department’s Psions die without helmets to be a temporary condition, not a design flaw. ¶ The Department believes it is in the right because it considers O.D.I.N. to be a European organisation that has excluded Russia from the world’s find sites for decades. In internal training sessions, it says: “The alloy belongs to those who find it. Not to those who administer it.”

### 06 Department 13 › Hooks and Outlook

- alt: White File “Chinese Whispers”. A dead drop in Murmansk contains a message in Russian: “The silence has a price.” The handler of Hold-5 considers it bait, but the Audit wants to know who wrote it. The cell is to watch the dead drop and find out who empties it.
- neu: White File “Chinese Whispers”. A dead drop in Murmansk contains a message in Russian: “Silence has a price.” The handler of Hold-5 considers it a lure, but the Audit wants to know who wrote it. The cell is to watch the dead drop and find out who empties it.

- alt: Red File “Kola-7”. The Audit has found an order of beryllium that went to a shell company in Murmansk. The cell is to travel to Kola and find out what the Department is building there. The journey leads into a restricted zone that O.D.I.N. has never seen.
- neu: Red File “Kola-7”. The Audit has found an order for beryllium that went to a shell company in Murmansk. The cell is to travel to Kola and find out what the Department is building there. The journey leads into a restricted zone that O.D.I.N. has never seen.

- alt: The next plan: “The Open Choir” ☠○○○○ . Department 13 wants to use its new helmets to build a Choir of its own, without the Rule of the Twelve, and so call the Thirteenth before O.D.I.N. does.
- neu: The next plan: “The Open Choir” ☠○○○○ . Department 13 wants to build its own choir with its new helmets, without the rule of the Twelve, and thereby call the Thirteenth before O.D.I.N. does.

### 10 Lodge of the Silent Circle › Relationship to O.D.I.N.

- alt: Who at O.D.I.N. deals with them. The Registrar in Stockholm, who has been conducting a quiet correspondence with the Master for years. Folio-7, who wanted to consult the church register in 2026 and was watched by the Lodge.
- neu: Who at O.D.I.N. deals with them. The Registrar in Stockholm, who has conducted a quiet correspondence with the Master for years. Folio-7, who wanted to inspect the church register in 2026 and was observed by the lodge.

- alt: What the Lodge knows about O.D.I.N. That the page lies in the second compartment of the steel cabinet. That the Registry is based in Stockholm. That Hallström found the page in 1938 and never handed it over. That O.D.I.N. guards the circles, but does not understand them.
- neu: What the Lodge knows about O.D.I.N. That the page lies in the second compartment of the steel cabinet. That the Registry is located in Stockholm. That Hallström found the page in 1938 and never released it. That O.D.I.N. guards the circles, but does not understand them.

- alt: The Lodge offers an agent knowledge. An answer to a question that O.D.I.N. does not answer. The tutor’s name, the song, the story of 1871. In return it wants a page from a file in the Registry. Or the missing page itself.
- neu: The Lodge offers an agent knowledge. An answer to a question that O.D.I.N. does not answer. The name of the tutor, the song, the story of 1871. In return it wants a page from a file of the Registry. Or the missing page itself.

### 10 Lodge of the Silent Circle › Bases and Means

- alt: The lodge house in Uppsala. Svartbäcksgatan 14, a narrow 19th-century house with wood panelling and a circle in the floor of the meeting room. There lie the lodge library with the minute book of 1872, the logbook of the ghost brig and the musical notation of the song, a meeting room with seven chairs and a cellar with a salt ring. Present are the Master, the Archivist, Martin Ekdahl and Alfred Sundberg. Henrik Gyllen comes once a month. You get in through the front door if you are invited as a guest, or through the back courtyard if you know the door to the cellar. The library is locked, the key hangs on the Archivist’s belt. ¶ The manor house at Lake Solberg. On the northern shore of the lake, a sanatorium even before 1937, closed off since 1938, today empty and dilapidated. There are the wall with the circles under the wallpaper, the dining hall in which the children sat in 1938, and in the cellar an old room with a salt ring. No one is there. The Forest Walker comes when the stone is moved. You get in through the bolted front door, through a window on the ground floor or via the old tradesmen’s entrance on the north side. ¶ The vicarage in Solberg. Next to the church, a red wooden house with a small archive. There lies the box of old papers, among them the newspaper article from 1872 and the pastor’s letter. The church register from 1841 to 1893 lies in the Uppsala regional archive. Present is Pastor Mattias Berg, 61, who wants to preserve the peace in the village. You get in through the front door if you give a reason. The archive is in the back room, the door is usually open. ¶ The Uppsala regional archive. A climate-controlled basement room in the Uppsala regional archive. There lies the church register of Solberg, 1841 to 1893, with the broken-off entry and the imprint of the missing page. A former lodge brother reports entries in the visitors’ book. You get in only with the archive’s permission, in white cotton gloves.

- alt: Money. A foundation account in Uppsala with about 200,000 euros, managed by the Master. The Lodge lives on the interest and on occasional sales from the library. ¶ People. Seven active members, all over 60 except Martin Ekdahl, no trained fighters. In addition, a network of former members in Sweden and Germany that supplies information.

### 10 Lodge of the Silent Circle › History and Goals

- alt: 1871, Lake Solberg. The children of the estate sit in a circle at night and sing an old song that the tutor taught them. In the morning the tutor and a child from the middle of the circle are gone. The pastor writes a page in the church register, then places it in the estate’s family Bible. ¶ January 1872, Uppsala. The lord of the manor, whose two children sat in the circle that night, founds the Lodge of the Silent Circle together with the pastor. The lord of the manor writes down the song as musical notation from his children’s memory. The Lodge receives a constitution and is affiliated to the federation.
- neu: 1871, Lake Solberg. The children of the estate sit in a circle at night and sing an old song that the tutor taught them. In the morning, the tutor and one child from the centre of the circle are gone. The pastor writes a page into the church register, then places it in the estate’s house Bible. ¶ January 1872, Uppsala. The lord of the manor, whose two children sat in the circle that night, founds the Lodge of the Silent Circle together with the pastor. The lord of the manor writes down the song as musical notation from his children’s memory. The lodge receives a charter and is affiliated with the federation.

- alt: 1938, Uppsala. Viktor Hallström finds the missing page in the estate’s family Bible. A lodge brother visits him in Stockholm. Afterwards Hallström writes nothing for three weeks. What was discussed, only the Lodge knows. Since that year the Lodge has been demanding the page back, because Hallström did not hand it over. ¶ 1957, Uppsala. Dr. Heinrich Sander comes back, barely aged. Hallström has Sander’s name overwritten in the visitors’ book of the regional archive. The Lodge knows whose name lies beneath the ink. It has never said. ¶ 1985, Stockholm. Hallström dies. The Audit collects his wall. With Hallström’s death the Lodge loses the trail to his hiding place. Only years later does it learn of the second compartment of the steel cabinet and begin to watch the Registry more closely. ¶ 2026, Uppsala. Folio-7 finds the imprint of the missing page in the church register. The Lodge notices his visit to the regional archive and begins to watch the Registry more closely. ¶ What only the game master knows. The Lodge has two skulls. It knows that the page lies in the second compartment of the steel cabinet. It is waiting for an opportunity to retrieve it.
- neu: 1938, Uppsala. Viktor Hallström finds the missing page in the estate’s house Bible. A lodge brother visits him in Stockholm. After that, Hallström writes nothing for three weeks. What was discussed, only the Lodge knows. Since that year, the lodge has demanded the page back, because Hallström did not hand it over. ¶ 1957, Uppsala. Dr. Heinrich Sander returns, barely aged. Hallström has Sander’s name overwritten in the visitors’ book of the regional archive. The Lodge knows whose name lies beneath the ink. It has never said. ¶ 1985, Stockholm. Hallström dies. The Audit collects his wall. With Hallström’s death the Lodge loses the trail to his hiding place. Only years later does it learn of the second compartment of the steel cabinet and begin to observe the Registry more closely. ¶ 2026, Uppsala. Folio-7 finds the imprint of the missing page in the church register. The lodge notices his visit to the regional archive and begins to observe the Registry more closely. ¶ What only the game master knows. The Lodge has two skulls. It knows that the page lies in the second compartment of the steel cabinet. It waits for an opportunity to retrieve it.

- alt: The Lodge wants to bring the missing page from the church register of 1871 back to Uppsala and put it back in the book. It believes that otherwise the book remains open, and that the circles are only silent when the page is in its place. The Lodge considers itself the guardian of a knowledge that no one else wants to preserve, and the last authority that knows the tutor’s name without misusing it.
- neu: The lodge wants to bring the missing page from the church register of 1871 back to Uppsala and put it back into the book. It believes that otherwise the book remains open, and that the circles only fall silent when the page is in its place. The lodge considers itself the custodian of knowledge that no one else wishes to preserve, and the final authority that knows the tutor’s name without misusing it.

### 10 Lodge of the Silent Circle › Overview

- alt: The Lodge of the Silent Circle is a small, old lodge in Uppsala that has preserved a song, a name and a debt since 1872. It works in the seventh degree, but has nothing to do with Freemasonry. It matters to the game master because it knows more about the night of 1871 than O.D.I.N. does, and because its plan to retrieve the missing page can change the world. ¶ “We never sang. We only listened, so that no one forgets that there was singing.”
- neu: The Lodge of the Silent Circle is a small, old lodge in Uppsala that has preserved a song, a name and a debt since 1872. It works in the seventh degree, but has nothing to do with Freemasonry. For the game master, it matters because it knows more about the night of 1871 than O.D.I.N. and because its plan to retrieve the missing page could change the world. ¶ “We never sang. We only listened, so that no one forgets that singing happened.

### 10 Lodge of the Silent Circle › Key Figures

- alt: Carl-Johan Rosenqvist, 78 years old, an old man with a white beard, a dark suit and a signet ring bearing a circle. He is the keeper of the song and of the name, and he wants the page back without O.D.I.N. smashing the Lodge. He knows the song of 1871, the tutor’s name and whose name was overwritten in the visitors’ book in 1957. His secret is that his predecessor helped Hallström hide Sander in 1957, and that he knows the truth from the Lodge’s minute book. He speaks deliberately, politely, in complete sentences, and asks questions instead of giving answers.
- neu: Carl-Johan Rosenqvist, 78 years old, an old man with a white beard, dark suit and a signet ring with a circle. He is the keeper of the song and the name, and he wants the page back without O.D.I.N. destroying the lodge. He knows the song of 1871, the name of the tutor and whose name was overwritten in the visitors’ book in 1957. His secret is that his predecessor helped Hallström hide Sander in 1957, and that he knows the truth from the lodge minute book. He speaks deliberately, politely, in complete sentences, and asks questions instead of giving answers.

- alt: Behaviour Waits, observes, tests. He gives only what one has earned, and he recognises who is lying.
- neu: Behaviour Waits, observes, tests. He gives only what one has earned, and he recognises who lies.

- alt: Elsa Norberg, 64 years old, a slender woman with a grey bun, glasses and a woollen cardigan that smells of paper. She wants to preserve the order of the library and protect the logbook from strangers’ hands. She knows the musical notation of the song, the story of the brig, the names of the founders and the circles from the logbook. Her secret is that in 2019 she sold a copy of the circles to an antiquarian in Hanover to finance the Lodge. The original is still there. She speaks quietly, precisely, with long pauses, and quotes from old books.

- alt: Behaviour Observes, takes notes, stays silent. She speaks only when the Master allows it.
- neu: Behaviour Observes, notes, remains silent. She speaks only when the Master allows it.

- alt: Martin Ekdahl, 35 years old, an athletic man with short blond hair and a suit that is one size too big. He wants to modernise the Lodge and win O.D.I.N. as a partner. He knows the constitution, the founding story and the names of the current members, but not the song. His secret is that in 2025 he told a Stockholm reporter about the Lodge, without naming names. The reporter works for an internet group called “Illuminati Europa”. He speaks quickly, enthusiastically, with many questions, and interrupts himself.

- alt: Special Has access to the Lodge’s rooms, but not to the library.
- neu: Special Has access to the lodge rooms, but not to the library.

- alt: Henrik Gyllen, 81 years old, a stooped old man with a walking stick who sits in the last row at every meeting and never speaks. He wants nothing more and is waiting for the end. He knows of the visit of 1938 from his father’s stories and from the minute book, the tutor’s name and the reason why the Lodge stays silent. His secret is that he is the great-grandson of the lord of the manor whose two children sat in the circle in 1871. One of the children was his grandmother. He does not speak at all. He nods, shakes his head, leaves.
- neu: Henrik Gyllen, 81 years old, a stooped old man with a cane who sits in the back row at every meeting and never speaks. He wants nothing more and waits for the end. His secret is that he is the great-grandson of the estate owner whose two children sat in the circle in 1871. One of the children was his grandmother. He does not speak at all. He nods, shakes his head, leaves.

- alt: Alfred Sundberg, 73 years old, an old man with round glasses and a briefcase full of books. He wants to retrieve the page from 1871, with O.D.I.N.’s help if necessary. He knows that the stone at Lake Solberg is old and that the Lodge preserves the song, and he knows the circles from the lodge library. His secret is that he deliberately had the runestone at Lake Solberg exposed in order to force O.D.I.N. to open the open file from 1871. He has not understood what he has woken. He speaks quietly, quotes from old books and avoids direct answers.

He speaks quietly, quotes from old books and avoids direct answers.

- alt: Special Knows the lodge library and its treasures. Has contacts with antiquarians all over Europe. ¶ Weakness His impatience. He wants the page so badly that he takes risks the Master would never approve. ¶ Behaviour Seems nervous, leafs through books, talks about old stones and circles. He sings the song secretly when he is alone.

### 10 Lodge of the Silent Circle › Hooks and Outlook

- alt: Grey File “The Tutor”. During construction work at Lake Solberg a runestone is found that bears the same circles as a wall in the old manor house. For a week the children in the village have been singing in their sleep. The cell is sent into the restricted zone as employees of a foundation for monument preservation to find out what the stone has woken, before a child disappears. ¶ Red File “The Page”. The Lodge has hired professionals from Grey Meridian to break into the Registry. The cell learns of it when a break-in into the Registry’s basement fails and a line of music is left behind at the crime scene. The Lodge will try again, and the cell must stop it before the page disappears.
- neu: Grey File “The Tutor”. During construction work at Solberg Lake, a rune stone is found that bears the same circles as a wall in the old manor house. For a week the children in the village have been singing in their sleep. The cell is sent into the restricted zone as employees of a foundation for monument preservation to find out what the stone has awakened before a child disappears. ¶ Red File “The Page”. The Lodge has hired professionals from Grey Meridian to break into the Registry. The cell learns of this when a break-in into the Registry’s basement fails and a line of music remains at the scene. The lodge will try again, and the cell must stop them before the page disappears.

- alt: The page lies in the church register in Uppsala again. The following night all children within a radius of 100 km sing in their sleep. The Lodge has reached its goal, but the song is louder than ever before. The Master closes the Lodge for three months and burns the copy of the musical notation. ¶ The next plan: “Keep the Song” ☠○○○○ . The Lodge wants to prevent the song from falling into the wrong hands, and begins to collect every copy it has ever sold.
- neu: The page lies in the church register in Uppsala again. The following night all children within a radius of 100 km sing in their sleep. The lodge has achieved its goal, but the song is louder than ever before. The Master closes the Lodge for three months and burns the copy of the musical notation. ¶ The next plan: “Keep the Song” ☠○○○○ . The lodge wants to prevent the song from falling into the wrong hands and begins to collect all copies it has ever sold.

- alt: File 6 “The Tutor” shows how the Lodge is entangled in this plan. Alfred Sundberg, the antiquarian from Uppsala, has the runestone at Lake Solberg exposed in order to force O.D.I.N. to open the open file from 1871. He hopes that in this way the Lodge will get the missing page back. If the cell unmasks Sundberg and secures the stone, the Lodge loses a skull. If Sundberg escapes with new knowledge about the whereabouts of the page, the Lodge gains a skull. In both cases the Lodge afterwards knows more about the cell than the cell knows about the Lodge.

He hopes the lodge will thus get the missing page back. If the cell exposes Sundberg and secures the stone, the lodge loses one skull. If Sundberg escapes with new knowledge of the page’s whereabouts, the lodge gains one skull. In both cases the Lodge afterwards knows more about the cell than the cell knows about the Lodge.

### 10 Lodge of the Silent Circle › The Plan: “The Page from 1871”

- alt: The Lodge wants to bring the missing page from the church register of 1871 back to Uppsala and put it back in the book. It believes that otherwise the book remains open and that the circles are only silent when the page is in its place. The cell first notices it in small things: a lodge brother in Stockholm asks after an agent, a dead drop contains a message with a circle, and at some point a young man in a suit that is too big for him stands in front of the handler and asks for a conversation.

The following night all children within a radius of 100 km sing in their sleep. Trail: A newspaper report about singing children in Sweden. The Singers gain a skull. ¶ The Key Figure is the Master of the Silent Circle, Carl-Johan Rosenqvist. He is the only one who knows the song of 1871 in full and is permitted to speak the tutor’s name. If the cell takes him out, the plan loses two skulls, and the Lodge does not roll after the next mission, because without its Master it makes no decisions. But the cell also loses the only source that could tell it the name from 1957 of its own free will.

- alt: 1 to 2 A witness disappears. The antiquarian in Hanover who works for the Lodge no longer turns up. His flat is empty, the books are gone. ¶ 3 to 4 A threat. The cell’s handler receives a call from Uppsala and hangs up without speaking. After that he gives the cell no more information about the Lodge.
